     9	} from "@/lib/mux-protocol";
    10	
    11	interface MuxContextValue {
    12	  subscribeTerminal: (
    13	    id: string,
    14	    callback: (data: string) => void,
    15	    projectId?: string,
    16	  ) => () => void;
    17	  writeTerminal: (id: string, data: string, projectId?: string) => void;
    18	  openTerminal: (id: string, projectId?: string, tmuxName?: string) => void;
    19	  closeTerminal: (id: string, projectId?: string) => void;
    20	  resizeTerminal: (id: string, cols: number, rows: number, projectId?: string) => void;
    21	  status: "connecting" | "connected" | "reconnecting" | "disconnected";
    22	  sessions: SessionPatch[];
    23	  notifications: DashboardNotificationRecord[];
    24	  notificationLimit: number;
    25	  /** Last session-fetch error from the server, null when healthy. */
    26	  lastError: string | null;
    27	  /** Last notification-store error from the server, null when healthy. */
    28	  notificationError: string | null;
    29	}
    30	
    31	const MuxContext = React.createContext<MuxContextValue | undefined>(undefined);
    32	
    33	function notificationKey(record: DashboardNotificationRecord): string {
    34	  return `${record.id}:${record.receivedAt}`;
    35	}
    36	
    37	function mergeNotifications(
    38	  current: DashboardNotificationRecord[],
    39	  appended: DashboardNotificationRecord[],
    40	  limit: number,
    41	): DashboardNotificationRecord[] {
    42	  const byKey = new Map<string, DashboardNotificationRecord>();
    43	  for (const record of current) {
    44	    byKey.set(notificationKey(record), record);
    45	  }
    46	  for (const record of appended) {
    47	    byKey.set(notificationKey(record), record);
    48	  }
    49	  return [...byKey.values()].slice(-limit);
    50	}
    51	
    52	export function useMux(): MuxContextValue {
    53	  const context = React.useContext(MuxContext);
    54	  if (!context) {
    55	    throw new Error("useMux() must be used within <MuxProvider>");
    56	  }
    57	  return context;
    58	}
    59	
    60	/** Like useMux() but returns undefined when outside a MuxProvider (safe for tests). */
    61	export function useMuxOptional(): MuxContextValue | undefined {
    62	  return React.useContext(MuxContext);
    63	}
    64	
    65	interface RuntimeTerminalConfig {
    66	  directTerminalPort?: unknown;
    67	  proxyWsPath?: unknown;
    68	}
    69	
    70	function normalizePortValue(value: unknown): string | undefined {
    71	  if (typeof value !== "string" && typeof value !== "number") return undefined;
    72	  const parsed = Number.parseInt(String(value), 10);
    73	  if (!Number.isInteger(parsed) || parsed <= 0 || parsed > 65535) return undefined;
    74	  return String(parsed);
    75	}
    76	
    77	function normalizePathValue(value: unknown): string | undefined {
    78	  if (typeof value !== "string") return undefined;
    79	  const trimmed = value.trim();
    80	  if (!trimmed.startsWith("/")) return undefined;
    81	  return trimmed;
    82	}
    83	
    84	function buildMuxWsUrl(runtimeConfig: {
    85	  directTerminalPort?: string;
    86	  proxyWsPath?: string;
    87	}): string {
    88	  const loc = window.location;
    89	  const protocol = loc.protocol === "https:" ? "wss:" : "ws:";
    90	
    91	  // Runtime proxy path takes priority (set by `ao start` via TERMINAL_WS_PATH env var)
    92	  const proxyWsPath = runtimeConfig.proxyWsPath ?? process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_TERMINAL_WS_PATH;
    93	  if (proxyWsPath) {
    94	    const basePath = proxyWsPath.replace(/\/ws\/?$/, "");
    95	    return `${protocol}//${loc.host}${basePath}/mux`;
    96	  }
    97	
    98	  // Port-less or standard ports: use path-based routing (reverse proxy expected)
    99	  if (loc.port === "" || loc.port === "443" || loc.port === "80") {
   100	    return `${protocol}//${loc.hostname}/ao-terminal-mux`;
   101	  }
   102	
   103	  // Direct port connection — prefer runtime-configured port, fall back to env/default
   104	  const port =
   105	    runtimeConfig.directTerminalPort ?? process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_DIRECT_TERMINAL_PORT ?? "14801";
   106	  return `${protocol}//${loc.hostname}:${port}/mux`;
   107	}
   108	
   109	function terminalKey(id: string, projectId?: string): string {
   110	  return projectId ? `${projectId}:${id}` : id;
   111	}
   112	
   113	export function MuxProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
   114	  const wsRef = useRef<WebSocket | null>(null);
   115	  const subscribersRef = useRef(new Map<string, Set<(data: string) => void>>());
   116	  const openedTerminalsRef = useRef(
   117	    new Map<string, { id: string; projectId?: string; tmuxName?: string }>(),
   118	  );
   119	  const [status, setStatus] = useState<
   120	    "connecting" | "connected" | "reconnecting" | "disconnected"
   121	  >("connecting");
   122	  const [sessions, setSessions] = useState<SessionPatch[]>([]);
   123	  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<DashboardNotificationRecord[]>([]);
   124	  const [notificationLimit, setNotificationLimit] = useState(50);
   125	  const [lastError, setLastError] = useState<string | null>(null);
   126	  const [notificationError, setNotificationError] = useState<string | null>(null);
   127	  const reconnectAttempt = useRef(0);
   128	  const reconnectTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
   129	  const runtimeConfigRef = useRef<{ directTerminalPort?: string; proxyWsPath?: string }>({});
   130	  const isDestroyedRef = useRef(false);
   131	
   132	  const connect = useCallback(() => {
   133	    if (wsRef.current) {
   134	      return;
   135	    }
   136	
   137	    setStatus("connecting");
   138	
   139	    try {
   140	      const url = buildMuxWsUrl(runtimeConfigRef.current);
   141	      console.log("[MuxProvider] Connecting to", url);
   142	      const ws = new WebSocket(url);
   143	      // Assign immediately so cleanup can close it even during CONNECTING state
   144	      wsRef.current = ws;
   145	
   146	      ws.addEventListener("open", () => {
   147	        if (isDestroyedRef.current) {
   148	          ws.close();
   149	          return;
   150	        }
   151	        console.log("[MuxProvider] Connected");
   152	        setStatus("connected");
   153	        reconnectAttempt.current = 0;
   154	
   155	        // Re-open previously opened terminals
   156	        for (const terminal of openedTerminalsRef.current.values()) {
   157	          const openMsg: ClientMessage = {
   158	            ch: "terminal",
   159	            id: terminal.id,
   160	            type: "open",
   161	            ...(terminal.projectId && { projectId: terminal.projectId }),
   162	            ...(terminal.tmuxName && { tmuxName: terminal.tmuxName }),
   163	          };
   164	          ws.send(JSON.stringify(openMsg));
   165	        }
   166	
   167	        // Always subscribe to sessions
   168	        const subMsg: ClientMessage = {
   169	          ch: "subscribe",
   170	          topics: ["sessions", "notifications"],
   171	        };
   172	        ws.send(JSON.stringify(subMsg));
   173	      });
   174	
   175	      ws.addEventListener("message", (event) => {
   176	        try {
   177	          const msg = JSON.parse(event.data as string) as ServerMessage;
   178	
   179	          if (msg.ch === "terminal") {
   180	            const key = terminalKey(msg.id, "projectId" in msg ? msg.projectId : undefined);
   181	            if (msg.type === "data") {
   182	              // Push to subscribers
   183	              const subs = subscribersRef.current.get(key);
   184	              if (subs) {
   185	                for (const callback of subs) {
   186	                  callback(msg.data);
   187	                }
   188	              }
   189	            } else if (msg.type === "opened") {
   190	              // Terminal opened successfully. Preserve any tmuxName stored by
   191	              // openTerminal so reconnects keep using the exact attach target.
   192	              if (!openedTerminalsRef.current.has(key)) {
   193	                openedTerminalsRef.current.set(key, {
   194	                  id: msg.id,
   195	                  ...("projectId" in msg && msg.projectId ? { projectId: msg.projectId } : {}),
   196	                });
   197	              }
   198	            } else if (msg.type === "exited") {
   199	              // PTY exited and could not be re-attached — remove so it isn't
   200	              // re-opened on reconnect, and surface a terminal-level error chunk
   201	              openedTerminalsRef.current.delete(key);
   202	              const subs = subscribersRef.current.get(key);
   203	              if (subs) {
   204	                const notice = `\r\n\x1b[31m[Terminal exited with code ${msg.code}]\x1b[0m\r\n`;
   205	                for (const callback of subs) {
   206	                  callback(notice);
   207	                }
   208	              }
   209	            } else if (msg.type === "error") {
   210	              console.error(`[MuxProvider] Terminal error for ${msg.id}:`, msg.message);
   211	            }
   212	          } else if (msg.ch === "sessions") {
   213	            if (msg.type === "snapshot") {
   214	              setSessions(msg.sessions);
   215	              setLastError(null);
   216	            } else if (msg.type === "error") {
   217	              setLastError(msg.error);
   218	            }
   219	          } else if (msg.ch === "notifications") {
   220	            if (msg.type === "snapshot") {
   221	              setNotificationLimit(msg.limit);
   222	              setNotifications(msg.notifications.slice(-msg.limit));
   223	              setNotificationError(null);
   224	            } else if (msg.type === "append") {
   225	              setNotificationLimit(msg.limit);
   226	              setNotifications((current) =>
   227	                mergeNotifications(current, msg.notifications, msg.limit),
   228	              );
   229	              setNotificationError(null);
   230	            } else if (msg.type === "error") {
   231	              setNotificationError(msg.error);
   232	            }
   233	          }
   234	        } catch (err) {
   235	          console.error("[MuxProvider] Error processing message:", err);
   236	        }
   237	      });
   238	
   239	      ws.addEventListener("error", (err) => {
   240	        console.error("[MuxProvider] WebSocket error:", err);
   241	      });
   242	
   243	      ws.addEventListener("close", () => {
   244	        console.log("[MuxProvider] Disconnected");
   245	        if (wsRef.current === ws) wsRef.current = null;
   246	
   247	        // Don't reconnect if the provider has been unmounted
   248	        if (isDestroyedRef.current) return;
   249	
   250	        // Reconnect with exponential backoff
   251	        const delayMs = Math.min(1000 * Math.pow(2, reconnectAttempt.current), 30_000);
   252	        reconnectAttempt.current += 1;
   253	        setStatus("reconnecting");
   254	
   255	        reconnectTimer.current = setTimeout(() => {
   256	          console.log(`[MuxProvider] Reconnecting (attempt ${reconnectAttempt.current})...`);
   257	          connect();
   258	        }, delayMs);
   259	      });
   260	    } catch (err) {
   261	      console.error("[MuxProvider] Failed to create WebSocket:", err);
   262	      setStatus("disconnected");
   263	    }
   264	  }, []);
   265	
   266	  // Fetch runtime config then connect. This ensures buildMuxWsUrl() has the
   267	  // server-configured port/path before the WebSocket is opened.
   268	  useEffect(() => {
   269	    // Reset destroyed flag so StrictMode double-invoke works correctly:
   270	    // cleanup sets it to true, but the re-run must treat itself as alive.
   271	    isDestroyedRef.current = false;
   272	    let cancelled = false;
   273	
   274	    const init = async () => {
   275	      try {
   276	        const res = await fetch("/api/runtime/terminal");
   277	        if (res.ok) {
   278	          const data = (await res.json()) as RuntimeTerminalConfig;
   279	          runtimeConfigRef.current = {
   280	            directTerminalPort: normalizePortValue(data.directTerminalPort),
   281	            proxyWsPath: normalizePathValue(data.proxyWsPath),
   282	          };
   283	        }
   284	      } catch {
   285	        // Ignore — fall back to env/default values
   286	      }
   287	      if (!cancelled) connect();
   288	    };
   289	
   290	    void init();
   291	
   292	    return () => {
   293	      cancelled = true;
   294	      isDestroyedRef.current = true;
   295	      if (reconnectTimer.current) {
   296	        clearTimeout(reconnectTimer.current);
   297	      }
   298	      wsRef.current?.close();
   299	    };
   300	  }, [connect]);
   301	
   302	  const subscribeTerminal = useCallback(
   303	    (id: string, callback: (data: string) => void, projectId?: string): (() => void) => {
   304	      const key = terminalKey(id, projectId);
   305	      // Add to subscribers
   306	      let subs = subscribersRef.current.get(key);
   307	      if (!subs) {
   308	        subs = new Set();
   309	        subscribersRef.current.set(key, subs);
   310	      }
   311	      subs.add(callback);
   312	
   313	      // Request open if not already open
   314	      if (!openedTerminalsRef.current.has(key) && wsRef.current?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
   315	        const openMsg: ClientMessage = {
   316	          ch: "terminal",
   317	          id,
   318	          type: "open",
   319	          ...(projectId && { projectId }),
   320	        };
   321	        wsRef.current.send(JSON.stringify(openMsg));
   322	      }
   323	
   324	      // Return unsubscribe function
   325	      return () => {
   326	        const subs = subscribersRef.current.get(key);
   327	        if (subs) {
   328	          subs.delete(callback);
   329	          if (subs.size === 0) {
   330	            subscribersRef.current.delete(key);
   331	          }
   332	        }
   333	      };
   334	    },
   335	    [],
   336	  );
   337	
   338	  const writeTerminal = useCallback((id: string, data: string, projectId?: string) => {
   339	    if (wsRef.current?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
   340	      const msg: ClientMessage = {
   341	        ch: "terminal",
   342	        id,
   343	        type: "data",
   344	        data,
   345	        ...(projectId && { projectId }),
   346	      };
   347	      wsRef.current.send(JSON.stringify(msg));
   348	    }
   349	  }, []);
   350	
   351	  const openTerminal = useCallback((id: string, projectId?: string, tmuxName?: string) => {
   352	    openedTerminalsRef.current.set(terminalKey(id, projectId), { id, projectId, tmuxName });
   353	    if (wsRef.current?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
   354	      const msg: ClientMessage = {
   355	        ch: "terminal",
   356	        id,
   357	        type: "open",
   358	        ...(projectId && { projectId }),
   359	        ...(tmuxName && { tmuxName }),
   360	      };
   361	      wsRef.current.send(JSON.stringify(msg));
   362	    }
   363	  }, []);
   364	
   365	  const closeTerminal = useCallback((id: string, projectId?: string) => {
   366	    openedTerminalsRef.current.delete(terminalKey(id, projectId));
   367	    if (wsRef.current?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
   368	      const msg: ClientMessage = {
   369	        ch: "terminal",
   370	        id,
   371	        type: "close",
   372	        ...(projectId && { projectId }),
   373	      };
   374	      wsRef.current.send(JSON.stringify(msg));
   375	    }
   376	  }, []);
   377	
   378	  const resizeTerminal = useCallback(
   379	    (id: string, cols: number, rows: number, projectId?: string) => {
   380	      if (wsRef.current?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
   381	        const msg: ClientMessage = {
   382	          ch: "terminal",
   383	          id,
   384	          type: "resize",
   385	          cols,
   386	          rows,
   387	          ...(projectId && { projectId }),
   388	        };
   389	        wsRef.current.send(JSON.stringify(msg));
   390	      }
   391	    },
   392	    [],
   393	  );
   394	
   395	  const contextValue: MuxContextValue = useMemo(
   396	    () => ({
   397	      subscribeTerminal,
   398	      writeTerminal,
   399	      openTerminal,
   400	      closeTerminal,
   401	      resizeTerminal,
   402	      status,
   403	      sessions,
   404	      notifications,
   405	      notificationLimit,
   406	      lastError,
   407	      notificationError,
   408	    }),
   409	    [
   410	      subscribeTerminal,
   411	      writeTerminal,
   412	      openTerminal,
   413	      closeTerminal,
   414	      resizeTerminal,
   415	      status,
   416	      sessions,
   417	      notifications,
   418	      notificationLimit,
   419	      lastError,
   420	      notificationError,
   421	    ],
   422	  );
   423	
   424	  return <MuxContext.Provider value={contextValue}>{children}</MuxContext.Provider>;
   425	}

> AGENT

AO’s terminal parity target is more than “can stream bytes”: it has explicit terminal controls, font-size persistence, follow-output detection with a jump-to-latest affordance, and mux lifecycle separation. `better-ao` already has the raw WebSocket attach path, so I’m going to focus first on the viewer UX gaps that don’t require changing AO’s runtime model.

     1	"use client";
     2	
     3	import { useState, type Dispatch, type SetStateAction } from "react";
     4	import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";
     5	import { FONT_SIZE_MAX, FONT_SIZE_MIN } from "./terminal-font";
     6	
     7	type MuxStatus = "connecting" | "connected" | "reconnecting" | "disconnected";
     8	type DisplayStatus = MuxStatus | "error";
     9	
    10	interface TerminalControlsProps {
    11	  sessionId: string;
    12	  chromeless: boolean;
    13	  isOpenCodeSession: boolean;
    14	  reloadCommand?: string;
    15	  fontSize: number;
    16	  setFontSize: Dispatch<SetStateAction<number>>;
    17	  fullscreen: boolean;
    18	  toggleFullscreen: () => void;
    19	  muxStatus: MuxStatus;
    20	  error: string | null;
    21	}
    22	
    23	export function TerminalControls({
    24	  sessionId,
    25	  chromeless,
    26	  isOpenCodeSession,
    27	  reloadCommand,
    28	  fontSize,
    29	  setFontSize,
    30	  fullscreen,
    31	  toggleFullscreen,
    32	  muxStatus,
    33	  error,
    34	}: TerminalControlsProps) {
    35	  const [reloading, setReloading] = useState(false);
    36	  const [reloadError, setReloadError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    37	
    38	  async function handleReload(): Promise<void> {
    39	    if (!isOpenCodeSession || reloading) return;
    40	    setReloadError(null);
    41	    setReloading(true);
    42	    try {
    43	      let commandToSend = reloadCommand;
    44	
    45	      if (!commandToSend) {
    46	        const remapRes = await fetch(`/api/sessions/${encodeURIComponent(sessionId)}/remap`, {
    47	          method: "POST",
    48	        });
    49	        if (!remapRes.ok) {
    50	          throw new Error(`Failed to remap OpenCode session: ${remapRes.status}`);
    51	        }
    52	        const remapData = (await remapRes.json()) as { opencodeSessionId?: unknown };
    53	        if (
    54	          typeof remapData.opencodeSessionId !== "string" ||
    55	          remapData.opencodeSessionId.length === 0
    56	        ) {
    57	          throw new Error("Missing OpenCode session id after remap");
    58	        }
    59	        commandToSend = `/exit\nopencode --session ${remapData.opencodeSessionId}\n`;
    60	      }
    61	
    62	      const sendRes = await fetch(`/api/sessions/${encodeURIComponent(sessionId)}/send`, {
    63	        method: "POST",
    64	        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    65	        body: JSON.stringify({ message: commandToSend }),
    66	      });
    67	      if (!sendRes.ok) {
    68	        throw new Error(`Failed to send reload command: ${sendRes.status}`);
    69	      }
    70	    } catch (err) {
    71	      setReloadError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to reload OpenCode session");
    72	    } finally {
    73	      setReloading(false);
    74	    }
    75	  }
    76	
    77	  // Local errors (e.g. xterm.js load failure) take priority over mux connection state
    78	  const displayStatus: DisplayStatus = error ? "error" : muxStatus;
    79	
    80	  const statusDotClass =
    81	    displayStatus === "connected"
    82	      ? "bg-[var(--color-status-ready)]"
    83	      : displayStatus === "error" || displayStatus === "disconnected"
    84	        ? "bg-[var(--color-status-error)]"
    85	        : "bg-[var(--color-status-attention)] animate-[pulse_1.5s_ease-in-out_infinite]";
    86	
    87	  const statusText =
    88	    displayStatus === "connected"
    89	      ? "Connected"
    90	      : displayStatus === "error"
    91	        ? (error ?? "Error")
    92	        : displayStatus === "disconnected"
    93	          ? "Disconnected"
    94	          : "Connecting…";
    95	
    96	  const statusTextColor =
    97	    displayStatus === "connected"
    98	      ? "text-[var(--color-status-ready)]"
    99	      : displayStatus === "error" || displayStatus === "disconnected"
   100	        ? "text-[var(--color-status-error)]"
   101	        : "text-[var(--color-text-tertiary)]";
   102	
   103	  const accentColor = "var(--color-accent)";
   104	
   105	  const fontSizeControls = (
   106	    <div className="flex items-center">
   107	      <button
   108	        onClick={() => setFontSize((prev) => Math.max(FONT_SIZE_MIN, prev - 1))}
   109	        disabled={fontSize <= FONT_SIZE_MIN}
   110	        className="w-5 h-5 text-xs flex items-center justify-center rounded hover:bg-white/10 disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-colors"
   111	        aria-label="Decrease font size"
   112	      >
   113	        −
   114	      </button>
   115	      <span className="w-9 text-center text-xs font-medium text-[var(--color-text-secondary)]">
   116	        {fontSize}px
   117	      </span>
   118	      <button
   119	        onClick={() => setFontSize((prev) => Math.min(FONT_SIZE_MAX, prev + 1))}
   120	        disabled={fontSize >= FONT_SIZE_MAX}
   121	        className="w-5 h-5 text-xs flex items-center justify-center rounded hover:bg-white/10 disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-colors"
   122	        aria-label="Increase font size"
   123	      >
   124	        +
   125	      </button>
   126	    </div>
   127	  );
   128	
   129	  const reloadButton = isOpenCodeSession ? (
   130	    <button
   131	      onClick={handleReload}
   132	      disabled={reloading || muxStatus !== "connected"}
   133	      title="Restart OpenCode session (/exit then resume mapped session)"
   134	      aria-label="Restart OpenCode session"
   135	      className="flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-[var(--color-text-tertiary)] transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--color-bg-subtle)] hover:text-[var(--color-text-primary)] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-70"
   136	    >

   138	        <>
   139	          <svg
   140	            className="h-3 w-3 animate-spin"
   141	            fill="none"
   142	            stroke="currentColor"
   143	            strokeWidth="2"
   144	            viewBox="0 0 24 24"
   145	          >
   146	            <path d="M12 3a9 9 0 109 9" />
   147	          </svg>
   148	          restarting
   149	        </>
   150	      ) : (
   151	        <>
   152	          <svg
   153	            className="h-3 w-3"
   154	            fill="none"
   155	            stroke="currentColor"
   156	            strokeWidth="2"
   157	            viewBox="0 0 24 24"
   158	          >
   159	            <path d="M21 12a9 9 0 11-2.64-6.36" />
   160	            <path d="M21 3v6h-6" />
   161	          </svg>
   162	          restart
   163	        </>
   164	      )}
   165	    </button>
   166	  ) : null;
   167	
   168	  const fullscreenButton = (
   169	    <button
   170	      onClick={toggleFullscreen}
   171	      className={cn(
   172	        "flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-[var(--color-text-tertiary)] transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--color-bg-subtle)] hover:text-[var(--color-text-primary)]",
   173	        !isOpenCodeSession && !chromeless && "ml-auto",
   174	      )}
   175	      aria-label={fullscreen ? "exit fullscreen" : "fullscreen"}
   176	    >
   177	      {fullscreen ? (
   178	        <>
   179	          <svg
   180	            className="h-3 w-3"
   181	            fill="none"
   182	            stroke="currentColor"
   183	            strokeWidth="2"
   184	            viewBox="0 0 24 24"
   185	          >
   186	            <path d="M8 3v3a2 2 0 01-2 2H3m18 0h-3a2 2 0 01-2-2V3m0 18v-3a2 2 0 012-2h3M3 16h3a2 2 0 012 2v3" />
   187	          </svg>
   188	          exit fullscreen
   189	        </>
   190	      ) : (
   191	        <>
   192	          <svg
   193	            className="h-3 w-3"
   194	            fill="none"
   195	            stroke="currentColor"
   196	            strokeWidth="2"
   197	            viewBox="0 0 24 24"
   198	          >
   199	            <path d="M8 3H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v3m18 0V5a2 2 0 00-2-2h-3m0 18h3a2 2 0 002-2v-3M3 16v3a2 2 0 002 2h3" />
   200	          </svg>
   201	          fullscreen
   202	        </>
   203	      )}
   204	    </button>
   205	  );
   206	
   207	  if (chromeless) {
   208	    return (
   209	      <div className="absolute right-3 top-3 z-10 flex items-center gap-1 rounded-[6px] border border-[var(--color-border-subtle)] bg-[color-mix(in_srgb,var(--color-bg-elevated)_92%,transparent)] px-1.5 py-1 shadow-[0_8px_24px_rgba(0,0,0,0.18)] backdrop-blur-sm">
   210	        {reloadButton}
   211	        {fullscreenButton}
   212	      </div>
   213	    );
   214	  }
   215	
   216	  return (
   217	    <div className="terminal-chrome-bar flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-[var(--color-border-subtle)] bg-[var(--color-bg-elevated)] px-3 py-2">
   218	      {/* Pane label — matches the workspace pane-header style used elsewhere */}
   219	      <span className="terminal-chrome-pane-label">TERMINAL</span>

   221	      <div className="terminal-chrome-identity">

   223	          {sessionId}
   224	        </span>
   225	        <div className="terminal-chrome-status-row">
   226	          <div className={cn("h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-full", statusDotClass)} />
   227	          <span
   228	            className={cn("text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.06em]", statusTextColor)}
   229	          >
   230	            {statusText}
   231	          </span>
   232	        </div>
   233	      </div>
   234	      <div className="flex-1" />
   235	      {fontSizeControls}
   236	      {reloadButton}
   237	      {reloadError ? (
   238	        <span
   239	          className="max-w-[40ch] truncate text-[10px] font-medium text-[var(--color-status-error)]"
   240	          title={reloadError}
   241	        >
   242	          {reloadError}
   243	        </span>
   244	      ) : null}
   245	      {fullscreenButton}
   246	    </div>
   247	  );
   248	}

     1	"use client";
     2	
     3	import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, type RefObject } from "react";
     4	import { useTheme } from "next-themes";
     5	
     6	// Import xterm CSS (must be imported in client component)
     7	import "@xterm/xterm/css/xterm.css";
     8	
     9	// Static type-only imports (erased at compile time, no SSR impact).
    10	// The runtime Terminal class is loaded via dynamic import() inside useEffect to avoid SSR.
    11	import type { Terminal as TerminalType } from "@xterm/xterm";
    12	import type { FitAddon as FitAddonType } from "@xterm/addon-fit";
    13	
    14	import { useMux } from "@/hooks/useMux";
    15	import { attachTouchScroll } from "@/lib/terminal-touch-scroll";
    16	
    17	import { registerClipboardHandlers } from "./terminal-clipboard";
    18	import { FONT_SIZE_KEY, resolveMonoFontFamily } from "./terminal-font";
    19	import { buildTerminalThemes, type TerminalVariant } from "./terminal-themes";
    20	
    21	export interface UseXtermTerminalOptions {
    22	  appearance: "theme" | "dark";
    23	  variant: TerminalVariant;
    24	  fontSize: number;
    25	  autoFocus: boolean;
    26	  projectId?: string;
    27	  /** Actual tmux session name. When provided, the terminal server uses it directly instead of resolving from sessionId. */
    28	  tmuxName?: string;
    29	}
    30	
    31	export interface UseXtermTerminalResult {
    32	  error: string | null;
    33	  followOutput: boolean;
    34	  scrollToLatest: () => void;
    35	  muxStatus: ReturnType<typeof useMux>["status"];
    36	  terminalInstance: RefObject<TerminalType | null>;
    37	  fitAddon: RefObject<FitAddonType | null>;
    38	}
    39	
    40	/**
    41	 * Owns the xterm.js instance and its wiring to the multiplexed WebSocket.
    42	 *
    43	 * Registers the XDA handler that tmux looks for to enable clipboard (OSC 52),
    44	 * buffers writes while a selection is active, preserves scrollback position
    45	 * for "follow output" behaviour, and tears everything down on unmount.
    46	 */
    47	export function useXtermTerminal(
    48	  terminalRef: RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>,
    49	  sessionId: string,
    50	  options: UseXtermTerminalOptions,
    51	): UseXtermTerminalResult {
    52	  const { appearance, variant, fontSize, autoFocus, projectId, tmuxName } = options;
    53	  const { resolvedTheme } = useTheme();
    54	  const terminalThemes = useMemo(() => buildTerminalThemes(variant), [variant]);
    55	  const {
    56	    subscribeTerminal,
    57	    writeTerminal,
    58	    resizeTerminal: resizeTerminalMux,
    59	    openTerminal,
    60	    closeTerminal,
    61	    status: muxStatus,
    62	  } = useMux();
    63	
    64	  const terminalInstance = useRef<TerminalType | null>(null);
    65	  const fitAddon = useRef<FitAddonType | null>(null);
    66	  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    67	  const followOutputRef = useRef(true);
    68	  const [followOutput, setFollowOutput] = useState(true);
    69	
    70	  useEffect(() => {
    71	    if (!terminalRef.current) return;
    72	    setError(null);
    73	
    74	    // Dynamically import xterm.js to avoid SSR issues
    75	    let mounted = true;
    76	    let cleanup: (() => void) | null = null;
    77	    let inputDisposable: { dispose(): void } | null = null;
    78	    let unsubscribe: (() => void) | null = null;
    79	
    80	    Promise.all([
    81	      import("@xterm/xterm").then((mod) => mod.Terminal),
    82	      import("@xterm/addon-fit").then((mod) => mod.FitAddon),
    83	      import("@xterm/addon-web-links").then((mod) => mod.WebLinksAddon),
    84	      document.fonts.ready,
    85	    ])
    86	      .then(([Terminal, FitAddon, WebLinksAddon]) => {
    87	        if (!mounted || !terminalRef.current) return;
    88	
    89	        const isDark = appearance === "dark" || resolvedTheme !== "light";
    90	        const activeTheme = isDark ? terminalThemes.dark : terminalThemes.light;
    91	
    92	        // NOTE: xterm's internal char-size measurement uses canvas ctx.font which
    93	        // cannot resolve `var(...)`. resolveMonoFontFamily() reads the CSS custom
    94	        // property at runtime so we still honour the app's configured font token
    95	        // (next/font generated name) while handing xterm a concrete string.
    96	        const terminal = new Terminal({
    97	          cursorBlink: true,
    98	          fontSize: fontSize,
    99	          fontFamily: resolveMonoFontFamily(),
   100	          // xterm v6 default lineHeight (1.0) collides rows with JetBrains Mono's
   101	          // tall x-height. 1.2 restores visual breathing room between lines.
   102	          lineHeight: 1.2,
   103	          theme: activeTheme,
   104	          // Light mode needs an explicit contrast floor because agent UIs often emit
   105	          // dim/faint ANSI sequences that become unreadable on a near-white background.
   106	          minimumContrastRatio: isDark ? 1 : 7,
   107	          // scrollback disabled — tmux provides scrollback/copy-mode, and leaving
   108	          // this > 0 makes FitAddon subtract DEFAULT_SCROLL_BAR_WIDTH (14px) from
   109	          // the available width, causing right-side clipping when the actual
   110	          // scrollbar is narrower or wider than assumed. Fixes #1677.
   111	          scrollback: 0,
   112	          allowProposedApi: true,
   113	          fastScrollSensitivity: 3,
   114	          scrollSensitivity: 1,
   115	        });

   117	        const fit = new FitAddon();
   118	        terminal.loadAddon(fit);
   119	        fitAddon.current = fit;
   120	
   121	        const webLinks = new WebLinksAddon();
   122	        terminal.loadAddon(webLinks);
   123	
   124	        registerClipboardHandlers(terminal);
   125	
   126	        terminal.open(terminalRef.current);
   127	        terminalInstance.current = terminal;
   128	
   129	        if (autoFocus) {

   131	        }
   132	
   133	        fit.fit();
   134	
   135	        // Deferred fit for containers that weren't sized yet on first paint.
   136	        const deferredFitTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
   137	          if (mounted && fitAddon.current) {
   138	            try {
   139	              fitAddon.current.fit();
   140	              resizeTerminalMux(sessionId, terminal.cols, terminal.rows, projectId);
   141	            } catch {
   142	              // Ignore fit errors
   143	            }
   144	          }
   145	        }, 100);
   146	
   147	        // next/font uses font-display:swap, so document.fonts.ready can resolve
   148	        // before JetBrains Mono actually swaps in. Without this listener,
   149	        // xterm's cell measurement stays pinned to the fallback font and cells
   150	        // look wide once the real font swaps. 'loadingdone' forces a re-fit.
   151	        const handleFontsLoadingDone = () => {
   152	          if (!mounted || !fitAddon.current || !terminalInstance.current) return;
   153	          try {
   154	            terminalInstance.current.options.fontFamily = resolveMonoFontFamily();
   155	            terminalInstance.current.clearTextureAtlas?.();
   156	            fitAddon.current.fit();
   157	            resizeTerminalMux(
   158	              sessionId,
   159	              terminalInstance.current.cols,
   160	              terminalInstance.current.rows,
   161	              projectId,
   162	            );
   163	          } catch {
   164	            // Ignore fit errors
   165	          }
   166	        };
   167	        // Feature-detect addEventListener — jsdom's document.fonts mock lacks it.
   168	        const fontsFace = typeof document !== "undefined" ? document.fonts : undefined;
   169	        const fontsListenerAttached =
   170	          !!fontsFace && typeof fontsFace.addEventListener === "function";
   171	        if (fontsListenerAttached) {
   172	          fontsFace!.addEventListener("loadingdone", handleFontsLoadingDone);
   173	        }
   174	
   175	        // Touch scroll on mobile — disables follow-output while user scrolls.
   176	        // NOTE: intentionally no onScrollTowardLatest. In normal buffer
   177	        // terminal.onScroll (below) decides based on real viewport position.
   178	        // In alternate buffer (tmux) there is no way to detect when the user
   179	        // returned to the live tail, so the jump-to-latest button stays visible
   180	        // until clicked.
   181	        const cleanupTouchScroll = attachTouchScroll(
   182	          terminal,
   183	          (data) => {
   184	            writeTerminal(sessionId, data, projectId);
   185	          },
   186	          {
   187	            onScrollAway: () => {
   188	              followOutputRef.current = false;
   189	              setFollowOutput(false);
   190	            },
   191	          },
   192	        );
   193	
   194	        let resizeObserver: ResizeObserver | null = null;
   195	        if (terminalRef.current) {
   196	          resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => {
   197	            if (mounted && fitAddon.current) {
   198	              try {
   199	                fitAddon.current.fit();
   200	                resizeTerminalMux(sessionId, terminal.cols, terminal.rows, projectId);
   201	              } catch {
   202	                // Ignore fit errors
   203	              }
   204	            }
   205	          });
   206	          resizeObserver.observe(terminalRef.current);
   207	        }
   208	
   209	        // Re-fit on devicePixelRatio changes (Windows display scaling, dragging
   210	        // window between monitors with different DPI). ResizeObserver doesn't
   211	        // fire for DPR-only changes, so we listen via matchMedia. Without this,
   212	        // moving the window to a 125%/150%-scaled monitor leaves a stripe of
   213	        // unrendered background to the right of the last column.
   214	        let dprMedia: MediaQueryList | null = null;
   215	        const handleDprChange = () => {
   216	          if (!mounted || !fitAddon.current || !terminalInstance.current) return;
   217	          try {
   218	            terminalInstance.current.clearTextureAtlas?.();
   219	            fitAddon.current.fit();
   220	            resizeTerminalMux(
   221	              sessionId,
   222	              terminalInstance.current.cols,
   223	              terminalInstance.current.rows,
   224	            );
   225	          } catch {
   226	            // Ignore fit errors
   227	          }
   228	        };

   231	          dprMedia.addEventListener?.("change", handleDprChange);
   232	        }

   234	        // ── Preserve selection while terminal receives output ────────
   235	        // xterm.js clears selection on every terminal.write(). We buffer
   236	        // incoming data while a selection is active so the highlight stays
   237	        // visible for Cmd+C. Flushed when selection clears or buffer caps.
   238	        const writeBuffer: string[] = [];
   239	        let selectionActive = false;
   240	        let safetyTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
   241	        let bufferBytes = 0;
   242	        const MAX_BUFFER_BYTES = 1_048_576; // 1 MB
   243	
   244	        const flushWriteBuffer = () => {
   245	          if (safetyTimer) {
   246	            clearTimeout(safetyTimer);
   247	            safetyTimer = null;
   248	          }
   249	          if (writeBuffer.length > 0) {
   250	            terminal.write(writeBuffer.join(""));
   251	            writeBuffer.length = 0;
   252	            bufferBytes = 0;
   253	          }
   254	        };
   255	
   256	        const selectionDisposable = terminal.onSelectionChange(() => {
   257	          if (terminal.hasSelection()) {
   258	            selectionActive = true;
   259	            if (!safetyTimer) {
   260	              safetyTimer = setTimeout(() => {
   261	                selectionActive = false;
   262	                flushWriteBuffer();
   263	              }, 5_000);
   264	            }
   265	          } else {
   266	            selectionActive = false;
   267	            flushWriteBuffer();
   268	          }
   269	        });

   271	        // Track whether our own write()-driven scrollToBottom() triggered the
   272	        // next scroll event. Declared before subscribeTerminal() so the
   273	        // callback's closure captures an initialised binding — not strictly
   274	        // required today (WebSocket callbacks are async) but removes the TDZ
   275	        // hazard if subscribeTerminal ever fires synchronously.
   276	        let programmaticScroll = false;

   281	          sessionId,
   282	          (data) => {

   286	              if (bufferBytes > MAX_BUFFER_BYTES) {
   287	                selectionActive = false;
   288	                flushWriteBuffer();
   289	              }

   297	          },

   299	        );

   302	        // scroll listener — xterm v6 may update scrollTop via RAF, making DOM
   303	        // "scroll" events unreliable for detecting user-initiated scrolls.
   304	        const scrollDisposable = terminal.onScroll(() => {

   306	            programmaticScroll = false;

   317	          }
   318	        });

   320	        const handleResize = () => {
   321	          if (fit) {

   324	          }

   328	        inputDisposable = terminal.onData((data) => {

   354	        console.error("[DirectTerminal] Failed to load xterm.js:", err);

   358	    return () => {
   359	      mounted = false;
   360	      cleanup?.();
   361	    };
   362	    // fontSize intentionally NOT in deps — it's handled by a dedicated effect
   363	    // below that mutates terminal.options.fontSize in place. Adding it here
   364	    // would tear down and recreate the terminal (and WebSocket) on every
   365	    // stepper click, causing a content flash and WebSocket reconnect.
   366	  }, [
   367	    appearance,
   368	    sessionId,
   369	    projectId,
   370	    tmuxName,
   371	    variant,
   372	    resolvedTheme,
   373	    terminalThemes,
   374	    subscribeTerminal,
   375	    writeTerminal,
   376	    resizeTerminalMux,
   377	    openTerminal,
   378	    closeTerminal,
   379	  ]);
   380	
   381	  // Re-send terminal dimensions on every reconnect so the server-side PTY
   382	  // matches the client's xterm.js size (new PTYs spawn at 80×24 default).

   384	    if (muxStatus !== "connected") return;

   386	    const terminal = terminalInstance.current;
   387	    if (!fit || !terminal) return;
   388	    fit.fit();
   389	    resizeTerminalMux(sessionId, terminal.cols, terminal.rows, projectId);
   390	  }, [muxStatus, sessionId, projectId, resizeTerminalMux]);

   392	  // Live theme switching without terminal recreation

   394	    const terminal = terminalInstance.current;

   398	    terminal.options.minimumContrastRatio = isDark ? 1 : 7;
   399	  }, [appearance, resolvedTheme, terminalThemes]);
   400	
   401	  // Font size change — mutate in place and persist, then resize the PTY so
   402	  // the shell wraps at the new cols/rows.
   403	  useEffect(() => {
   404	    const terminal = terminalInstance.current;
   405	    const fit = fitAddon.current;
   406	    if (!terminal || !fit) return;
   407	    terminal.options.fontSize = fontSize;
   408	    try {
   409	      localStorage.setItem(FONT_SIZE_KEY, String(fontSize));
   410	    } catch {
   411	      // localStorage might be unavailable
   412	    }
   413	    fit.fit();
   414	    resizeTerminalMux(sessionId, terminal.cols, terminal.rows, projectId);
   415	  }, [fontSize, sessionId, projectId, resizeTerminalMux]);
   416	
   417	  const scrollToLatest = () => {

   417	  const scrollToLatest = () => {
   418	    const t = terminalInstance.current;
   419	    if (t) {
   420	      if (t.buffer.active.type === "normal") {
   421	        // Normal buffer: xterm scrollback is disabled (scrollback: 0), so
   422	        // history lives in tmux. scrollToBottom() is a safe no-op that
   423	        // re-anchors the viewport to the live tail.
   424	        t.scrollToBottom();
   425	      } else {
   426	        // Alternate buffer (tmux/vim): the user is in tmux copy-mode
   427	        // (entered by attachTouchScroll on swipe). Send 'q' to exit
   428	        // copy-mode and return to live tail.
   429	        writeTerminal(sessionId, "q", projectId);
   430	      }
   431	    }
   432	    followOutputRef.current = true;
   433	    setFollowOutput(true);
   434	  };
   435	
   436	  return {
   437	    error,

          'border-border bg-background text-negative-600 shadow-xs hover:border-transparent hover:bg-destructive/90 hover:text-destructive-foreground dark:text-negative-400 dark:hover:text-destructive-foreground [a:hover_&]:bg-destructive/90 [a:hover_&]:text-destructive-foreground dark:[a:hover_&]:bg-destructive/90 dark:[a:hover_&]:text-destructive-foreground [button:hover_&]:bg-destructive/90 [button:hover_&]:text-destructive-foreground dark:[button:hover_&]:bg-destructive/90 dark:[button:hover_&]:text-destructive-foreground',

        link: 'underline-offset-4 hover:underline [a:hover_&]:underline [button:hover_&]:underline',
      },
      size: {
        default: 'h-9 px-4 has-[>span>svg]:px-3',
        xs: "h-6 rounded-sm px-2 text-xs has-[>span>svg]:px-1.5 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-3 [&>span]:gap-1",
        sm: 'h-8 px-3 has-[>span>svg]:px-2.5',
        lg: 'h-10 px-6 has-[>span>svg]:px-4',
        icon: 'size-9 max-w-none',
        'icon-xs':
          "size-6 max-w-none rounded-sm text-xs [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-3",
        'icon-sm': 'size-8 max-w-none',
        'icon-lg': 'size-10 max-w-none',
      },
    },
    compoundVariants: [
      {
        variant: ['link'],
        size: ['xs', 'sm', 'default', 'lg'],
        class: 'px-0 has-[>span>svg]:px-0',
      },
    ],
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: 'default',
      size: 'default',
    },
  }
);

type ButtonProps = ButtonPrimitive.Props &
  VariantProps<typeof buttonVariants> & {
    loading?: boolean;
  };

function Button({
  className,
  children,
  variant,
  size,
  disabled,
  loading,
  ...props
}: ButtonProps) {
  return (
    <ButtonPrimitive
      data-slot="button"
      className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size, className }))}
      disabled={loading || disabled}
      {...props}
    >
      {!!loading && (
        <span className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
          <Spinner />
        </span>
      )}
      <span
        className={cn(
          'flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center justify-center',
          loading && 'opacity-0'
        )}
      >
        {children}
      </span>
    </ButtonPrimitive>
  );
}

export { Button, buttonVariants };

import { Tooltip as TooltipPrimitive } from '@base-ui/react/tooltip';

import { cn } from '@/lib/tailwind/utils';

function TooltipProvider({
  delay = 500,
  ...props
}: TooltipPrimitive.Provider.Props) {
  return (
    <TooltipPrimitive.Provider
      data-slot="tooltip-provider"
      delay={delay}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

function Tooltip({ ...props }: TooltipPrimitive.Root.Props) {
  return (
    <TooltipProvider>
      <TooltipPrimitive.Root data-slot="tooltip" {...props} />
    </TooltipProvider>
  );
}

function TooltipTrigger({ ...props }: TooltipPrimitive.Trigger.Props) {
  return <TooltipPrimitive.Trigger data-slot="tooltip-trigger" {...props} />;
}

function TooltipContent({
  className,
  side = 'top',
  sideOffset = 8,
  align = 'center',
  alignOffset = 0,
  children,
  ...props
}: TooltipPrimitive.Popup.Props &
  Pick<
    TooltipPrimitive.Positioner.Props,
    'align' | 'alignOffset' | 'side' | 'sideOffset'
  >) {
  return (
    <TooltipPrimitive.Portal>
      <TooltipPrimitive.Positioner
        align={align}
        alignOffset={alignOffset}
        side={side}
        sideOffset={sideOffset}
        className="isolate z-50"
      >
        <TooltipPrimitive.Popup
          data-slot="tooltip-content"
          className={cn(
            'z-50 w-fit max-w-xs origin-(--transform-origin) rounded-md bg-foreground px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-background data-closed:animate-out data-closed:fade-out-0 data-closed:zoom-out-95 data-open:animate-in data-open:fade-in-0 data-open:zoom-in-95 data-[side=bottom]:slide-in-from-top-2 data-[side=inline-end]:slide-in-from-left-2 data-[side=inline-start]:slide-in-from-right-2 data-[side=left]:slide-in-from-right-2 data-[side=right]:slide-in-from-left-2 data-[side=top]:slide-in-from-bottom-2 data-[state=delayed-open]:animate-in data-[state=delayed-open]:fade-in-0 data-[state=delayed-open]:zoom-in-95',
            className
          )}
          {...props}
        >
          {children}
          <TooltipPrimitive.Arrow className="z-50 size-2.5 translate-y-[calc(-50%-2px)] rotate-45 rounded-[2px] bg-foreground fill-foreground data-[side=bottom]:top-1 data-[side=inline-end]:top-1/2! data-[side=inline-end]:-left-1 data-[side=inline-end]:-translate-y-1/2 data-[side=inline-start]:top-1/2! data-[side=inline-start]:-right-1 data-[side=inline-start]:-translate-y-1/2 data-[side=left]:top-1/2! data-[side=left]:-right-1 data-[side=left]:-translate-y-1/2 data-[side=right]:top-1/2! data-[side=right]:-left-1 data-[side=right]:-translate-y-1/2 data-[side=top]:-bottom-2.5" />
        </TooltipPrimitive.Popup>
      </TooltipPrimitive.Positioner>
    </TooltipPrimitive.Portal>
  );
}

export { Tooltip, TooltipContent, TooltipProvider, TooltipTrigger };

# @wterm/react

React component for [wterm](https://github.com/vercel-labs/wterm) — a terminal emulator for the web.

## Install

```bash
npm install @wterm/dom @wterm/react
```

## Usage

```tsx
import { Terminal } from "@wterm/react";
import "@wterm/react/css";

function App() {
  return <Terminal />;
}
```

By default, typed input is echoed back to the terminal. Use `onData` with `useTerminal` when you need control over input:

```tsx
import { Terminal, useTerminal } from "@wterm/react";
import "@wterm/react/css";

function App() {
  const { ref, write } = useTerminal();

  return (
    <Terminal
      ref={ref}
      onData={(data) => {
        socket.send(data);
      }}
    />
  );
}
```

The WASM binary is embedded in the package — no extra setup required. To serve it separately instead, pass `wasmUrl`.

## `<Terminal>` Props

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `cols` | `number` | `80` | Initial column count |
| `rows` | `number` | `24` | Initial row count |
| `wasmUrl` | `string` | — | Optional URL to serve the WASM binary separately (embedded by default) |
| `theme` | `string` | — | Theme name (e.g. `"solarized-dark"`, `"monokai"`, `"light"`) |
| `autoResize` | `boolean` | `false` | Auto-resize based on container dimensions |
| `cursorBlink` | `boolean` | `false` | Enable cursor blinking animation |
| `debug` | `boolean` | `false` | Enable debug mode. Exposes a `DebugAdapter` on the underlying `WTerm` instance for inspecting escape sequences, cell data, render performance, and unhandled CSI sequences. |
| `onData` | `(data: string) => void` | — | Called when the terminal produces data (user input or host response). When omitted, input is echoed back automatically. |
| `onTitle` | `(title: string) => void` | — | Called when the terminal title changes |
| `onResize` | `(cols: number, rows: number) => void` | — | Called on resize |
| `onReady` | `(wt: WTerm) => void` | — | Called after WASM is loaded and the terminal is initialized |

Standard `div` props (`className`, `style`, `id`, etc.) are forwarded to the container element.

## `useTerminal` Hook

Returns a ref and imperative helpers for controlling the terminal:

```tsx
const { ref, write, resize, focus } = useTerminal();
```

| Return | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `ref` | `React.RefObject<TerminalHandle>` | Pass to `<Terminal ref={ref}>` |
| `write` | `(data: string \| Uint8Array) => void` | Write data to the terminal |
| `resize` | `(cols: number, rows: number) => void` | Resize the terminal |
| `focus` | `() => void` | Focus the terminal |

## `TerminalHandle`

The imperative handle exposed via `ref`:

```ts
interface TerminalHandle {
  write(data: string | Uint8Array): void;
  resize(cols: number, rows: number): void;
  focus(): void;
  readonly instance: WTerm | null;
}
```

## Themes

Import the stylesheet to get the default theme and all built-in themes:

```tsx
import "@wterm/react/css";
```

Switch themes via the `theme` prop:

```tsx
<Terminal theme="monokai" />
```

Built-in: `solarized-dark`, `monokai`, `light`. Define custom themes with CSS custom properties.

## License

Apache-2.0

import { jsx as _jsx } from "react/jsx-runtime";
import { useRef, useCallback, useImperativeHandle, forwardRef, } from "react";
import { WTerm } from "@wterm/dom";
const Terminal = forwardRef(function Terminal({ cols = 80, rows = 24, core, wasmUrl, theme, autoResize = false, cursorBlink = false, debug = false, onData, onTitle, onResize, onReady, onError, className, style, ...htmlProps }, ref) {
    const wtermRef = useRef(null);
    const callbacksRef = useRef({
        onData,
        onTitle,
        onResize,
        onReady,
        onError,
    });
    const autoResizeRef = useRef(autoResize);
    callbacksRef.current = { onData, onTitle, onResize, onReady, onError };
    autoResizeRef.current = autoResize;
    useImperativeHandle(ref, () => ({
        write(data) {
            wtermRef.current?.write(data);
        },
        resize(c, r) {
            wtermRef.current?.resize(c, r);
        },
        focus() {
            wtermRef.current?.focus();
        },
        get instance() {
            return wtermRef.current;
        },
    }));
    // React 19 callback ref with cleanup — replaces useEffect for
    // imperative, non-React library init that requires a DOM element.
    const containerRef = useCallback((el) => {
        if (!el)
            return;
        const wt = new WTerm(el, {
            cols,
            rows,
            core,
            wasmUrl,
            autoResize: autoResizeRef.current,
            cursorBlink,
            debug,
            onData: callbacksRef.current.onData
                ? (data) => callbacksRef.current.onData?.(data)
                : undefined,
            onTitle: (title) => callbacksRef.current.onTitle?.(title),
            onResize: (c, r) => callbacksRef.current.onResize?.(c, r),
        });
        wtermRef.current = wt;
        wt.init()
            .then(() => {
            callbacksRef.current.onReady?.(wt);
        })
            .catch((err) => {
            if (callbacksRef.current.onError) {
                callbacksRef.current.onError(err);
            }
            else {
                console.error(err);
            }
        });
        return () => {
            wt.destroy();
            wtermRef.current = null;
        };
    }, 
    // Re-run only when the WASM source changes
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    [core, wasmUrl]);
    // Sync props to the existing instance (render-time checks)
    const wt = wtermRef.current;
    if (wt?.bridge) {
        if (!autoResizeRef.current && (wt.cols !== cols || wt.rows !== rows)) {
            wt.resize(cols, rows);
        }
        const el = wt.element;
        if (cursorBlink && !el.classList.contains("cursor-blink")) {
            el.classList.add("cursor-blink");
        }
        else if (!cursorBlink && el.classList.contains("cursor-blink")) {
            el.classList.remove("cursor-blink");
        }
        if (onData && !wt.onData) {
            wt.onData = (data) => callbacksRef.current.onData?.(data);
        }
        else if (!onData && wt.onData) {
            wt.onData = null;
        }
    }
    const themeClass = theme ? `theme-${theme}` : "";
    const classes = ["wterm", themeClass, className].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
    const mergedStyle = {
        ...(autoResize ? undefined : { height: rows * 17 + 24 }),
        ...style,
    };
    return (_jsx("div", { ref: containerRef, className: classes || undefined, style: mergedStyle, role: "textbox", "aria-label": "Terminal", "aria-multiline": "true", "aria-roledescription": "terminal", ...htmlProps }));
});
export default Terminal;

//# sourceMappingURL=wterm.d.ts.mapimport { WasmBridge } from "@wterm/core";
import { Renderer } from "./renderer.js";
import { InputHandler } from "./input.js";
import { DebugAdapter } from "./debug.js";
export class WTerm {
    constructor(element, options = {}) {
        this.bridge = null;
        this.debug = null;
        this.renderer = null;
        this.input = null;
        this.rafId = null;
        this._renderTimer = null;
        this.resizeObserver = null;
        this._destroyed = false;
        this._shouldScrollToBottom = false;
        this._rowHeight = 0;
        this.element = element;
        this._coreOption = options.core;
        this.wasmUrl = options.wasmUrl;
        this.cols = options.cols || 80;
        this.rows = options.rows || 24;
        this.autoResize = options.autoResize !== false;
        this._debugEnabled = options.debug ?? false;
        this.onData = options.onData || null;
        this.onTitle = options.onTitle || null;
        this.onResize = options.onResize || null;
        this._container = document.createElement("div");
        this._container.className = "term-grid";
        this.element.appendChild(this._container);
        this.element.classList.add("wterm");
        if (options.cursorBlink)
            this.element.classList.add("cursor-blink");
        this._onClickFocus = () => {
            const sel = window.getSelection();
            if (!sel || sel.isCollapsed)
                this.input?.focus();
        };
        this.element.addEventListener("click", this._onClickFocus);
    }
    async init() {
        try {
            if (this._coreOption) {
                this.bridge = this._coreOption;
            }
            else {
                this.bridge = await WasmBridge.load(this.wasmUrl);
            }
            if (this._destroyed)
                return this;
            this.bridge.init(this.cols, this.rows);
            if (this._debugEnabled) {
                this.debug = new DebugAdapter();
                this.debug.setBridge(this.bridge);
                globalThis.__wterm = this;
            }
            this._setRowHeight();
            this.renderer = new Renderer(this._container);
            this.renderer.setup(this.cols, this.rows);
            this.input = new InputHandler(this.element, (data) => {
                this._scrollToBottom();
                if (this.onData) {
                    this.onData(data);
                }
                else {
                    this.write(data);
                }
            }, () => this.bridge);
            if (this.autoResize) {

    const markTerminalConnectionStableLater = () => {
      clearStableConnectionTimer();
      stableConnectionTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
        stableConnectionTimer = undefined;
        reconnectAttemptRef.current = 0;
      }, terminalStableConnectionMs);
    };

    const scheduleTerminalReconnect = () => {
      if (reconnectTimer !== undefined) {
        return;
      }

      const nextDelay = terminalReconnectDelaysMs[reconnectAttemptRef.current];
      if (nextDelay === undefined) {
        return;
      }

      reconnectAttemptRef.current += 1;
      reconnectTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
        reconnectTimer = undefined;
        clearBeforeNextMessageRef.current = true;
        setConnectionAttempt((currentAttempt) => currentAttempt + 1);
      }, nextDelay);
    };

    const socket = new WebSocket(
      createTerminalWebSocketURL(
        { id: sessionId, project: sessionProject },
        window.location,
        terminalSize
      )
    );
    socket.binaryType = 'arraybuffer';
    socketRef.current = socket;
    setConnectionStatus('connecting');

    socket.addEventListener('open', () => {
      if (!active) {
        return;
      }

      clearReconnectTimer();
      markTerminalConnectionStableLater();
      setConnectionStatus('connected');
      syncTerminalSize({ forceSend: true });
    });

    socket.addEventListener('message', (event) => {
      if (!active) {
        return;
      }

      markTerminalConnectionUseful();
      const currentTerminal = terminalRef.current;
      if (!currentTerminal) {
        return;
      }

      if (clearBeforeNextMessageRef.current) {
        currentTerminal.write(terminalResetSequence);
        clearBeforeNextMessageRef.current = false;
      }
      writeTerminalMessage(currentTerminal, event.data);
    });

    socket.addEventListener('error', () => {
      if (active) {
        clearStableConnectionTimer();
        setConnectionStatus('failed');
        scheduleTerminalReconnect();
      }
    });

    socket.addEventListener('close', (event) => {
      if (!active) {
        return;
      }

      if (event.wasClean) {
        clearStableConnectionTimer();
        setConnectionStatus('disconnected');
        return;
      }

      clearStableConnectionTimer();
      setConnectionStatus('failed');
      scheduleTerminalReconnect();
    });

    return () => {
      active = false;
      if (socketRef.current === socket) {
        socketRef.current = null;
      }

      clearStableConnectionTimer();
      clearReconnectTimer();
      socket.close(1000, 'terminal session changed');
    };
  }, [
    connectionAttempt,
    isTerminalReadyForSession,
    sessionId,
    sessionProject,
    sessionTerminalSupported,
    syncTerminalSize,
  ]);

  const handleTerminalReady = useCallback(
    (terminal: WTerm) => {
      terminalRef.current = terminal;

      if (isTerminalReadyForSession) {
        scheduleTerminalSizeSync({ forceSend: true });
        return;
      }

      const measuredSize = measureTerminalSize(terminal.element);
      if (measuredSize) {
        terminalSizeRef.current = measuredSize;
        if (
          terminal.cols !== measuredSize.cols ||
          terminal.rows !== measuredSize.rows
        ) {
          terminal.resize(measuredSize.cols, measuredSize.rows);
        }
      }
      setReadyTerminalSessionKey(terminalSessionKey);
    },
    [isTerminalReadyForSession, scheduleTerminalSizeSync, terminalSessionKey]
  );

  const handleTerminalData = useCallback((data: string) => {
    const socket = socketRef.current;
    if (!socket || socket.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) {
      return;
    }

    socket.send(textEncoder.encode(data));
  }, []);

  const handleTerminalResize = useCallback(
    (cols: number, rows: number) => {
      applyTerminalSize({ cols, rows });
    },
    [applyTerminalSize]
  );

  const handleTerminalError = useCallback((error: unknown) => {
    console.error('wterm failed to initialize', error);
    setConnectionStatus('failed');
  }, []);

  const handleTerminalRetry = useCallback(() => {
    clearBeforeNextMessageRef.current = true;
    reconnectAttemptRef.current = 0;
    setConnectionAttempt((currentAttempt) => currentAttempt + 1);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!sessionId || !sessionWorkerId) {
      lastConnectionToastRef.current = undefined;
      return;
    }

    const toastKey = `${sessionId}:${connectionAttempt}:${connectionStatus}`;
    if (lastConnectionToastRef.current === toastKey) {
      return;
    }

    switch (connectionStatus) {
      case 'unsupported':
        toast.warning('Terminal unavailable', {
          description: `${sessionWorkerId} does not expose an attachable runtime.`,
        });
        lastConnectionToastRef.current = toastKey;
        return;
      case 'disconnected':
        toast.warning('Terminal disconnected', {
          action: {
            label: 'Reconnect',
            onClick: handleTerminalRetry,
          },
          description: `${sessionWorkerId} stopped streaming terminal output.`,
        });
        lastConnectionToastRef.current = toastKey;
        return;
      case 'failed':
        toast.error('Terminal connection failed', {
          action: {
            label: 'Reconnect',
            onClick: handleTerminalRetry,
          },
          description: `${sessionWorkerId} could not attach to the worker runtime.`,
        });
        lastConnectionToastRef.current = toastKey;
        return;
      default:
        return;
    }
  }, [
    connectionAttempt,
    connectionStatus,
    handleTerminalRetry,
    sessionId,
    sessionWorkerId,
  ]);

  const handleTerminalFullscreenToggle = useCallback(() => {
    const terminalPanel = terminalPanelRef.current;

    if (!terminalPanel) {
      return;
    }

    if (document.fullscreenElement === terminalPanel) {
      void document.exitFullscreen();
      return;
    }

    void terminalPanel.requestFullscreen();
  }, []);

  const terminalLabel = session
    ? session.kind === 'orchestrator'
      ? 'Orchestrator terminal'
      : `${session.workerId} terminal`
    : 'Worker terminal';
  const fullscreenButtonLabel = isTerminalFullscreen
    ? 'Restore terminal'
    : 'Maximize terminal';
  const canRetry = isRetryableTerminalStatus(connectionStatus);

  return (
    <section
      ref={terminalPanelRef}
      aria-label={`${terminalLabel} panel`}
      aria-hidden={props.hidden ? true : undefined}
      data-acceptance-anchor="terminal-panel"
      hidden={props.hidden}
      className={cn(
        'relative flex min-h-90 min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col border-b border-border bg-background md:min-h-0 md:border-b-0',
        isTerminalFullscreen && 'h-dvh w-dvw border-b-0',
        props.className
      )}
    >
      <div
        ref={terminalViewportRef}
        className={cn(
          'min-h-0 flex-1 bg-background p-3',
          isTerminalFullscreen && 'p-0'
        )}
      >
        <Terminal
          key={terminalSessionKey}
          aria-label={terminalLabel}
          autoResize
          className="ao-terminal"
          cols={initialTerminalSize.cols}
          cursorBlink
          onData={handleTerminalData}
          onError={handleTerminalError}
          onReady={handleTerminalReady}
          onResize={handleTerminalResize}
          rows={initialTerminalSize.rows}
          wasmUrl="/wterm.wasm"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="absolute top-3 right-3 flex items-center">
        {canRetry ? (
          <IconButton label="Reconnect terminal" onClick={handleTerminalRetry}>
            <RotateCcwIcon />
          </IconButton>
        ) : null}
        <OpenIdeButton session={session} />
        <IconButton
          label={fullscreenButtonLabel}
          onClick={handleTerminalFullscreenToggle}
        >
          {isTerminalFullscreen ? <Minimize2Icon /> : <Maximize2Icon />}
        </IconButton>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function measureTerminalSize(element: HTMLElement): TerminalSize | undefined {
  const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
  if (rect.width <= 0 || rect.height <= 0) {
    return undefined;
  }

  const row = document.createElement('div');
  row.className = 'term-row';
  row.style.position = 'absolute';
  row.style.visibility = 'hidden';
  row.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
  row.style.whiteSpace = 'pre';
  row.style.top = '0';
  row.style.left = '0';

  const probe = document.createElement('span');
  probe.textContent = 'W';
  row.appendChild(probe);
  element.appendChild(row);

  const charWidth = probe.getBoundingClientRect().width;
  const rowHeight = row.getBoundingClientRect().height;
  row.remove();

  if (charWidth <= 0 || rowHeight <= 0) {
    return undefined;
  }

  const style = getComputedStyle(element);
  const paddingX =
    toPixelNumber(style.paddingLeft) + toPixelNumber(style.paddingRight);
  const paddingY =
    toPixelNumber(style.paddingTop) + toPixelNumber(style.paddingBottom);
  const availableWidth = element.clientWidth - paddingX;
  const availableHeight = element.clientHeight - paddingY;

  if (availableWidth <= 0 || availableHeight <= 0) {
    return undefined;
  }

  return {
    cols: Math.max(1, Math.floor(availableWidth / charWidth)),
    rows: Math.max(1, Math.floor(availableHeight / rowHeight)),
  };
}

function toPixelNumber(value: string) {
  const parsed = Number.parseFloat(value);
  return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : 0;
}

function IconButton(props: {
  label: string;
  children: ReactNode;
  onClick?: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <Tooltip>
      <TooltipTrigger
        render={
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="secondary"

function writeTerminalMessage(terminal: WTerm, data: unknown) {
  if (typeof data === 'string') {
    terminal.write(data);
    return;
  }

  if (data instanceof ArrayBuffer) {
    terminal.write(new Uint8Array(data));
    return;
  }

  if (data instanceof Blob) {
    void data.arrayBuffer().then((buffer) => {
      terminal.write(new Uint8Array(buffer));
    });
  }
}

import type { WorkerSession } from '@/features/home/domain/session-workspace';

export const initialTerminalSize = {
  cols: 100,
  rows: 30,
};

export type TerminalConnectionStatus =
  | 'idle'
  | 'connecting'
  | 'connected'
  | 'disconnected'
  | 'failed'
  | 'unsupported';

export interface TerminalLocation {
  host: string;
  protocol: string;
}

export function createTerminalWebSocketURL(
  session: Pick<WorkerSession, 'id' | 'project'>,
  location: TerminalLocation,
  size = initialTerminalSize
) {
  const protocol = location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss:' : 'ws:';
  const url = new URL(
    `/api/sessions/${encodeURIComponent(session.id)}/terminal`,
    `${protocol}//${location.host}`
  );

  url.searchParams.set('cols', String(size.cols));
  url.searchParams.set('project', session.project);
  url.searchParams.set('rows', String(size.rows));

  return url.toString();
}

export function isRetryableTerminalStatus(status: TerminalConnectionStatus) {
  return status === 'disconnected' || status === 'failed';
}

function measureTerminalSize(element: HTMLElement): TerminalSize | undefined {
  const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
  if (rect.width <= 0 || rect.height <= 0) {
    return undefined;
  }

  const row = document.createElement('div');
  row.className = 'term-row';
  row.style.position = 'absolute';
  row.style.visibility = 'hidden';
  row.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
  row.style.whiteSpace = 'pre';
  row.style.top = '0';
  row.style.left = '0';

  const probe = document.createElement('span');
  probe.textContent = 'W';
  row.appendChild(probe);
  element.appendChild(row);

  const charWidth = probe.getBoundingClientRect().width;

    return (terminalWindow.__terminalSentPayloads ?? []).flatMap((payload) => {

        if (

async function sendTerminalBackendText(page: Page, text: string) {
  await page.evaluate((message) => {
    const terminalWindow = window as Window & {
      __terminalWebSockets?: Array<
        EventTarget & {
          messageListenerCount?: number;
          pushBackendText?: (message: string) => void;
          readyState?: number;
          url?: string;
        }
      >;
    };
    const socket = terminalWindow.__terminalWebSockets
      ?.filter(
        (candidate) =>
          candidate.url?.includes('/api/sessions/') &&
          candidate.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN &&
          (candidate.messageListenerCount ?? 0) > 0
      )
      .at(-1);

    if (!socket) {
      throw new Error('No fake terminal websocket is connected.');
    }
    if (!socket.pushBackendText) {
      throw new Error('Fake terminal websocket cannot push backend text.');
    }

    socket.pushBackendText(message);
  }, text);
}

async function sendTerminalBackendTextAndWait(
  page: Page,
  terminalPanel: Locator,
  text: string,
  expectedText: string
) {
  const terminal = terminalPanel.locator('.ao-terminal');

  await expect
    .poll(
      async () => {
        await sendTerminalBackendText(page, text);
        await page.waitForTimeout(100);
        return (await terminal.textContent()) ?? '';
      },
      {
        timeout: 15_000,
      }
    )
    .toContain(expectedText);
}

async function dropTerminalWebSocket(page: Page) {
  await page.evaluate(() => {
    const terminalWindow = window as Window & {
      __terminalWebSockets?: Array<
        EventTarget & {
          fail?: () => void;
          messageListenerCount?: number;
          readyState?: number;
          url?: string;
        }
      >;
    };
    const socket = terminalWindow.__terminalWebSockets
      ?.filter(
        (candidate) =>
          candidate.url?.includes('/api/sessions/') &&
          candidate.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN &&
          (candidate.messageListenerCount ?? 0) > 0
      )
      .at(-1);

    if (!socket?.fail) {
      throw new Error('No fake terminal websocket can be dropped.');
    }

    socket.fail();
  });
}

function waitForHomeWorkspacePreferences(page: Page) {
  return expect
    .poll(() =>
      page.evaluate(() =>
        window.localStorage.getItem('better-ao.home.workspace-preferences')
      )
    )
    .not.toBeNull();
}

test('terminal output can be scrolled with the browser wheel', async ({
  page,
}) => {
  await installFakeTerminalWebSocket(page);
  await page.route('**/api/workspace', async (route) => {
    await route.fulfill({
      body: JSON.stringify({
        activeProjectId: 'agent-orchestrator_live',
        projects: [
          { id: 'agent-orchestrator_live', name: 'Agent Orchestrator' },
        ],
        sessions: [
          {
            agent: 'codex',
            cwd: '/tmp/ao-live',
            description: 'A selected live worker.',
            id: 'ao-live',
            issue: '[PR #1]',
            metadata: '[codex/working]',
            project: 'agent-orchestrator_live',
            selected: true,
            state: 'working',
            terminalSupported: true,
            title: 'Live worker',
            workerId: '[AO-LIVE]',
          },
        ],
      }),
      contentType: 'application/json',
      status: 200,
    });
  });

  await page.goto('/');
  await expect(
    page.getByRole('button', { name: '[AO-LIVE] Live worker' })
  ).toBeVisible({
    timeout: 15_000,
  });

  await page.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Terminal' }).click();
  const terminalPanel = page.locator(
    '[data-acceptance-anchor="terminal-panel"]'
  );
  const terminal = terminalPanel.getByRole('textbox');
  await expect(terminal).toBeVisible();
  await expect.poll(() => getTerminalOpenWebSocketCount(page)).not.toBe(0);
  await expect.poll(() => hasTerminalResizeControlMessage(page)).toBe(true);

  const scrollbackText = Array.from(
    { length: 120 },
    (_, index) => `SCROLL_${index.toString().padStart(3, '0')}\r\n`
  ).join('');
  await sendTerminalBackendTextAndWait(
    page,
    terminalPanel,
    scrollbackText,
    'SCROLL_119'
  );

  await expect
    .poll(() =>
      terminal.evaluate(
        (element) => element.scrollHeight > element.clientHeight
      )
    )
    .toBe(true);
  await terminal.evaluate((element) => {
    element.scrollTop = 0;
  });
  await terminal.hover();
  await page.mouse.wheel(0, 400);
  await expect
    .poll(() => terminal.evaluate((element) => element.scrollTop))
    .toBeGreaterThan(0);
});

test('terminal can jump back to latest output after scrolling away', async ({
  page,
}) => {
  await installFakeTerminalWebSocket(page);
  await page.route('**/api/workspace', async (route) => {
    await route.fulfill({
      body: JSON.stringify({
        activeProjectId: 'agent-orchestrator_live',
        projects: [
          { id: 'agent-orchestrator_live', name: 'Agent Orchestrator' },
        ],
        sessions: [
          {
            agent: 'codex',
            cwd: '/tmp/ao-live',
            description: 'A selected live worker.',
            id: 'ao-live',
            issue: '[PR #1]',
            metadata: '[codex/working]',
            project: 'agent-orchestrator_live',
            selected: true,
            state: 'working',
            terminalSupported: true,
            title: 'Live worker',
            workerId: '[AO-LIVE]',
          },
        ],
      }),
      contentType: 'application/json',
      status: 200,
    });
  });

  await page.goto('/');
  await expect(
    page.getByRole('button', { name: '[AO-LIVE] Live worker' })
  ).toBeVisible({
    timeout: 15_000,
  });

  await page.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Terminal' }).click();
  const terminalPanel = page.locator(
    '[data-acceptance-anchor="terminal-panel"]'
  );
  const terminal = terminalPanel.getByRole('textbox');
  await expect(terminal).toBeVisible();
  await expect.poll(() => getTerminalOpenWebSocketCount(page)).not.toBe(0);

  const scrollbackText = Array.from(
    { length: 120 },
    (_, index) => `LATEST_${index.toString().padStart(3, '0')}\r\n`
  ).join('');
  await sendTerminalBackendTextAndWait(
    page,
    terminalPanel,
    scrollbackText,
    'LATEST_119'
  );

  await expect
    .poll(() =>
      terminal.evaluate(
        (element) => element.scrollHeight > element.clientHeight
      )
    )
    .toBe(true);
  await terminal.evaluate((element) => {
    element.scrollTop = 0;
    element.dispatchEvent(new Event('scroll'));
  });

  await expect(
    terminalPanel.getByRole('button', { name: 'Jump to latest' })
  ).toBeVisible();
  await terminalPanel.getByRole('button', { name: 'Jump to latest' }).click();

  await expect
    .poll(() =>
      terminal.evaluate(
        (element) =>
          element.scrollHeight - element.clientHeight - element.scrollTop
      )
    )
    .toBeLessThanOrEqual(8);
  await expect(
    terminalPanel.getByRole('button', { name: 'Jump to latest' })
  ).toBeHidden();
});

test('terminal font size controls persist across reloads', async ({ page }) => {
  await installFakeTerminalWebSocket(page);
  await page.route('**/api/workspace', async (route) => {
    await route.fulfill({
      body: JSON.stringify({
        activeProjectId: 'agent-orchestrator_live',
        projects: [
          { id: 'agent-orchestrator_live', name: 'Agent Orchestrator' },
        ],
        sessions: [
          {
            agent: 'codex',
            cwd: '/tmp/ao-live',
            description: 'A selected live worker.',
            id: 'ao-live',
            issue: '[PR #1]',
            metadata: '[codex/working]',
            project: 'agent-orchestrator_live',
            selected: true,
            state: 'working',
            terminalSupported: true,
            title: 'Live worker',
            workerId: '[AO-LIVE]',
          },
        ],
      }),
      contentType: 'application/json',
      status: 200,
    });
  });

  await page.goto('/');
  await expect(
    page.getByRole('button', { name: '[AO-LIVE] Live worker' })
  ).toBeVisible({
    timeout: 15_000,
  });

  await page.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Terminal' }).click();
  const terminalPanel = page.locator(
    '[data-acceptance-anchor="terminal-panel"]'
  );
  const terminalFontSize = terminalPanel.getByLabel('Terminal font size', {
    exact: true,
  });
  await expect(terminalPanel.getByText('Connected')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(terminalFontSize).toHaveText('12px');

  await terminalPanel
    .getByRole('button', { name: 'Increase terminal font size' })
    .click();
  await expect(terminalFontSize).toHaveText('13px');
  await expect
    .poll(() =>
      terminalPanel
        .locator('.ao-terminal')
        .evaluate((element) =>
          getComputedStyle(element).getPropertyValue('--term-font-size').trim()
        )
    )
    .toBe('13px');

  await page.reload();
  await expect(
    page.getByRole('button', { name: '[AO-LIVE] Live worker' })
  ).toBeVisible({
    timeout: 15_000,
  });
  await page.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Terminal' }).click();
  await expect(
    page
      .locator('[data-acceptance-anchor="terminal-panel"]')
      .getByLabel('Terminal font size', { exact: true })
  ).toHaveText('13px');
});
