import { expect, type Locator, type Page, test } from '@playwright/test';

async function installFakeTerminalWebSocket(page: Page) {
  await page.addInitScript(() => {
    class FakeTerminalWebSocket extends EventTarget {
      static CONNECTING = 0;
      static OPEN = 1;
      static CLOSING = 2;
      static CLOSED = 3;

      binaryType = 'blob';
      messageListenerCount = 0;
      readyState = FakeTerminalWebSocket.CONNECTING;
      url: string;

      constructor(url: string) {
        super();
        this.url = url;
        const terminalWindow = window as Window & {
          __terminalAutoFailWebSockets?: boolean;
          __terminalInitialBackendText?: string;
          __terminalSentPayloads?: Array<number[] | string>;
          __terminalWebSockets?: FakeTerminalWebSocket[];
          __terminalWebSocketUrls?: string[];
        };
        terminalWindow.__terminalSentPayloads =
          terminalWindow.__terminalSentPayloads ?? [];
        terminalWindow.__terminalWebSockets = [
          ...(terminalWindow.__terminalWebSockets ?? []),
          this,
        ];
        terminalWindow.__terminalWebSocketUrls = [
          ...(terminalWindow.__terminalWebSocketUrls ?? []),
          url,
        ];
        window.setTimeout(() => {
          if (this.readyState !== FakeTerminalWebSocket.CONNECTING) {
            return;
          }

          this.readyState = FakeTerminalWebSocket.OPEN;
          this.dispatchEvent(new Event('open'));
          this.pushInitialBackendTextIfReady();
          if (terminalWindow.__terminalAutoFailWebSockets) {
            window.setTimeout(() => this.fail(), 0);
          }
        }, 0);
      }

      addEventListener(
        type: string,
        callback: EventListenerOrEventListenerObject | null,
        options?: AddEventListenerOptions | boolean
      ) {
        if (type === 'message' && callback) {
          this.messageListenerCount += 1;
          this.pushInitialBackendTextIfReady();
        }

        super.addEventListener(type, callback, options);
      }

      removeEventListener(
        type: string,
        callback: EventListenerOrEventListenerObject | null,
        options?: EventListenerOptions | boolean
      ) {
        if (type === 'message' && callback) {
          this.messageListenerCount = Math.max(
            0,
            this.messageListenerCount - 1
          );
        }

        super.removeEventListener(type, callback, options);
      }

      close() {
        this.readyState = FakeTerminalWebSocket.CLOSED;
        this.dispatchEvent(new CloseEvent('close', { wasClean: true }));
      }

      fail() {
        this.readyState = FakeTerminalWebSocket.CLOSED;
        this.dispatchEvent(new CloseEvent('close', { wasClean: false }));
      }

      pushBackendText(message: string) {
        this.dispatchEvent(
          new MessageEvent('message', {
            data: new TextEncoder().encode(message).buffer,
          })
        );
      }

      pushInitialBackendTextIfReady() {
        if (
          this.readyState !== FakeTerminalWebSocket.OPEN ||
          this.messageListenerCount === 0
        ) {
          return;
        }

        const terminalWindow = window as Window & {
          __terminalInitialBackendText?: string;
        };
        const initialBackendText = terminalWindow.__terminalInitialBackendText;
        if (!initialBackendText) {
          return;
        }

        terminalWindow.__terminalInitialBackendText = undefined;
        window.setTimeout(() => this.pushBackendText(initialBackendText), 0);
      }

      send(payload: string | Uint8Array) {
        const terminalWindow = window as Window & {
          __terminalSentPayloads?: Array<number[] | string>;
        };
        terminalWindow.__terminalSentPayloads =
          terminalWindow.__terminalSentPayloads ?? [];

        if (payload instanceof Uint8Array) {
          terminalWindow.__terminalSentPayloads.push(Array.from(payload));
          this.dispatchEvent(
            new MessageEvent('message', {
              data: new TextDecoder().decode(payload),
            })
          );
          return;
        }

        terminalWindow.__terminalSentPayloads.push(payload);
      }
    }

    Object.defineProperty(window, 'WebSocket', {
      configurable: true,
      value: FakeTerminalWebSocket,
    });
  });
}

function getTerminalWebSocketCount(page: Page) {
  return page.evaluate(
    () =>
      (
        window as Window & {
          __terminalWebSocketUrls?: string[];
        }
      ).__terminalWebSocketUrls?.filter((url) => url.includes('/api/sessions/'))
        .length ?? 0
  );
}

function getLatestTerminalWebSocketURL(page: Page) {
  return page.evaluate(() => {
    const urls =
      (
        window as Window & {
          __terminalWebSocketUrls?: string[];
        }
      ).__terminalWebSocketUrls ?? [];

    return urls.filter((url) => url.includes('/api/sessions/')).at(-1);
  });
}

function getTerminalOpenWebSocketCount(page: Page) {
  return page.evaluate(
    () =>
      (
        window as Window & {
          __terminalWebSockets?: Array<{
            messageListenerCount?: number;
            readyState?: number;
            url?: string;
          }>;
        }
      ).__terminalWebSockets?.filter(
        (socket) =>
          socket.url?.includes('/api/sessions/') &&
          socket.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN &&
          (socket.messageListenerCount ?? 0) > 0
      ).length ?? 0
  );
}

function setTerminalWebSocketsAutoFail(page: Page, enabled: boolean) {
  return page.evaluate((shouldAutoFail) => {
    (
      window as Window & {
        __terminalAutoFailWebSockets?: boolean;
      }
    ).__terminalAutoFailWebSockets = shouldAutoFail;
  }, enabled);
}

function getTerminalSentText(page: Page) {
  return page.evaluate(() => {
    const terminalWindow = window as Window & {
      __terminalSentPayloads?: Array<number[] | string>;
    };
    const decoder = new TextDecoder();

    return (terminalWindow.__terminalSentPayloads ?? [])
      .map((payload) =>
        Array.isArray(payload)
          ? decoder.decode(new Uint8Array(payload))
          : payload
      )
      .join('');
  });
}

function hasTerminalResizeControlMessage(page: Page) {
  return page.evaluate(() => {
    const terminalWindow = window as Window & {
      __terminalSentPayloads?: Array<number[] | string>;
    };

    return (terminalWindow.__terminalSentPayloads ?? []).some((payload) => {

          parsed.cols > 0 &&
          typeof parsed.rows === 'number' &&
          parsed.rows > 0
        );
      } catch {
        return false;
      }
    });
  });
}

test('terminal resizes the attached PTY with the viewport', async ({
  page,
}) => {
  await installFakeTerminalWebSocket(page);
  await page.setViewportSize({ height: 900, width: 1440 });
  await page.route('**/api/workspace', async (route) => {
    await route.fulfill({
      body: JSON.stringify({
        activeProjectId: 'agent-orchestrator_live',
        projects: [
          { id: 'agent-orchestrator_live', name: 'Agent Orchestrator' },
        ],
        sessions: [
          {
            agent: 'codex',
            cwd: '/tmp/ao-live',
            description: 'A selected live worker.',
            id: 'ao-live',
            issue: '[PR #1]',
            metadata: '[codex/working]',
            project: 'agent-orchestrator_live',
            selected: true,
            state: 'working',
            terminalSupported: true,
            title: 'Live worker',
            workerId: '[AO-LIVE]',
          },
        ],
      }),
      contentType: 'application/json',
      status: 200,
    });
  });

  await page.goto('/');
  await expect(
    page.getByRole('button', { name: '[AO-LIVE] Live worker' })
  ).toBeVisible({
    timeout: 15_000,
  });

  await page.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Terminal' }).click();
  await expect(
    page
      .locator('[data-acceptance-anchor="terminal-panel"]')
      .getByRole('textbox')
  ).toBeVisible();
  await expect.poll(() => getTerminalOpenWebSocketCount(page)).not.toBe(0);
  await expect.poll(() => hasTerminalResizeControlMessage(page)).toBe(true);

  const websocketURL = await getLatestTerminalWebSocketURL(page);
  if (!websocketURL) {
    throw new Error('Terminal websocket URL was not captured.');
  }

  const initialURLCols = Number(new URL(websocketURL).searchParams.get('cols'));
  expect(initialURLCols).toBeGreaterThan(100);

  const initialResizeMessages = await getTerminalResizeControlMessages(page);
  const initialResize = initialResizeMessages.at(-1);
  if (!initialResize) {
    throw new Error('Terminal resize control message was not captured.');
  }

  await page.setViewportSize({ height: 650, width: 900 });
  await expect
    .poll(async () => {
      const latestResize = (await getTerminalResizeControlMessages(page)).at(
        -1
      );
      return latestResize ? latestResize.cols < initialResize.cols : false;
    })
    .toBe(true);

  page,
}) => {

  await page.route('**/api/workspace', async (route) => {
    await route.fulfill({

  );

  await dropTerminalWebSocket(page);
  await expect
    .poll(() => getTerminalWebSocketCount(page))
    .toBeGreaterThan(initialWebSocketCount);
  await expect.poll(() => getTerminalOpenWebSocketCount(page)).not.toBe(0);
  await sendTerminalBackendTextAndWait(
    page,
    terminalPanel,
    'BEFORE_DROP\r\nAFTER_RECONNECT\r\n',
    'AFTER_RECONNECT'
  );
  await expect
    .poll(async () => {
      const text = (await terminalPanel.textContent()) ?? '';
      return text.match(/BEFORE_DROP/g)?.length ?? 0;
    })
    .toBe(1);
});

import { Terminal, type WTerm } from '@wterm/react';
import {
  ArrowDownIcon,
  Maximize2Icon,
  Minimize2Icon,
  MinusIcon,
  PlusIcon,
  RotateCcwIcon,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  type CSSProperties,
  type ReactNode,
  useCallback,
  useEffect,
  useLayoutEffect,
  useRef,
  useState,
} from 'react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

import { cn } from '@/lib/tailwind/utils';

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import {
  Tooltip,
  TooltipContent,
  TooltipTrigger,
} from '@/components/ui/tooltip';

import { OpenIdeButton } from '@/features/home/components/molecules/open-ide-button';
import {
  createTerminalWebSocketURL,
  initialTerminalSize,
  isRetryableTerminalStatus,
  type TerminalConnectionStatus,
} from '@/features/home/components/organisms/terminal-connection';
import type { WorkerSession } from '@/features/home/domain/session-workspace';

const textEncoder = new TextEncoder();
const terminalReconnectDelaysMs = [500, 1_000, 2_000] as const;
const terminalResetSequence = '\x1b[3J\x1b[2J\x1b[H';
const terminalStableConnectionMs = 5_000;
const terminalFontSizeStorageKey = 'better-ao.terminal.fontSize';
const terminalDefaultFontSize = 12;
const terminalMinFontSize = 10;
const terminalMaxFontSize = 18;
const terminalFollowThresholdPx = 8;

type TerminalSize = typeof initialTerminalSize;

export function TerminalPanel(props: {
  className?: string;
  hidden?: boolean;
  session?: WorkerSession;
}) {
  const { session } = props;
  const socketRef = useRef<WebSocket | null>(null);
  const terminalRef = useRef<WTerm | null>(null);
  const terminalPanelRef = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null);
  const terminalViewportRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const clearBeforeNextMessageRef = useRef(false);
  const reconnectAttemptRef = useRef(0);
  const pendingTerminalResizeFrameRef = useRef<number | undefined>(undefined);
  const pendingTerminalResizeForceRef = useRef(false);
  const pendingTerminalFollowFrameRef = useRef<number | undefined>(undefined);
  const terminalSizeRef = useRef(initialTerminalSize);
  const lastConnectionToastRef = useRef<string | undefined>(undefined);
  const isFollowingOutputRef = useRef(true);
  const [readyTerminalSessionKey, setReadyTerminalSessionKey] = useState<
    string | undefined
  >(undefined);
  const [connectionStatus, setConnectionStatus] =
    useState<TerminalConnectionStatus>('idle');
  const [connectionAttempt, setConnectionAttempt] = useState(0);
  const [isTerminalFullscreen, setIsTerminalFullscreen] = useState(false);
  const [terminalFontSize, setTerminalFontSize] = useState(
    getInitialTerminalFontSize
  );
  const [isFollowingOutput, setIsFollowingOutput] = useState(true);

  const sendResize = useCallback((cols: number, rows: number) => {
    const socket = socketRef.current;
    if (!socket || socket.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) {
      return;
    }

    socket.send(
      JSON.stringify({
        cols,
        rows,
        type: 'resize',
      })
    );
  }, []);

  const terminalSessionKey = session
    ? `${session.project}/${session.id}`
    : 'idle';
  const sessionId = session?.id;
  const sessionProject = session?.project;
  const sessionTerminalSupported = session?.terminalSupported;
  const sessionWorkerId = session?.workerId;
  const isTerminalReadyForSession =
    readyTerminalSessionKey === terminalSessionKey;

  const applyTerminalSize = useCallback(
    (size: TerminalSize, options?: { forceSend?: boolean }) => {
      const previousSize = terminalSizeRef.current;
      terminalSizeRef.current = size;

      const terminal = terminalRef.current;
      if (
        terminal &&
        isTerminalReadyForSession &&
        (terminal.cols !== size.cols || terminal.rows !== size.rows)
      ) {
        terminal.resize(size.cols, size.rows);
      }

      if (
        options?.forceSend ||
        previousSize.cols !== size.cols ||
        previousSize.rows !== size.rows
      ) {
        sendResize(size.cols, size.rows);
      }
    },
    [isTerminalReadyForSession, sendResize]
  );

  const syncTerminalSize = useCallback(
    (options?: { forceSend?: boolean }) => {
      const terminal = terminalRef.current;
      if (!terminal || !isTerminalReadyForSession) {
        return false;
      }

      const measuredSize = measureTerminalSize(terminal.element);
      if (!measuredSize) {
        return false;
      }

      applyTerminalSize(measuredSize, options);
      return true;
    },
    [applyTerminalSize, isTerminalReadyForSession]
  );

  const scheduleTerminalSizeSync = useCallback(
    (options?: { forceSend?: boolean }) => {
      pendingTerminalResizeForceRef.current =
        pendingTerminalResizeForceRef.current || Boolean(options?.forceSend);

      if (pendingTerminalResizeFrameRef.current !== undefined) {
        return;
      }

      pendingTerminalResizeFrameRef.current = window.requestAnimationFrame(
        () => {
          pendingTerminalResizeFrameRef.current = undefined;
          const forceSend = pendingTerminalResizeForceRef.current;
          pendingTerminalResizeForceRef.current = false;
          syncTerminalSize({ forceSend });
        }
      );
    },
    [syncTerminalSize]
  );

  const setTerminalFollowingOutput = useCallback((nextValue: boolean) => {
    isFollowingOutputRef.current = nextValue;
    setIsFollowingOutput((currentValue) =>
      currentValue === nextValue ? currentValue : nextValue
    );
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleFullscreenChange = () => {
      setIsTerminalFullscreen(
        document.fullscreenElement === terminalPanelRef.current
      );

      scheduleTerminalSizeSync({ forceSend: true });
    };

    document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', handleFullscreenChange);

    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('fullscreenchange', handleFullscreenChange);
    };
  }, [scheduleTerminalSizeSync]);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const terminal = terminalRef.current;
    if (!terminal || !isTerminalReadyForSession) {
      return;
    }

    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => {
      scheduleTerminalSizeSync();
    });
    resizeObserver.observe(terminal.element);

    const terminalViewport = terminalViewportRef.current;
    if (terminalViewport) {
      resizeObserver.observe(terminalViewport);
    }

    const handleViewportResize = () => {
      scheduleTerminalSizeSync();
    };

    const handleTerminalScroll = () => {
      setTerminalFollowingOutput(isTerminalAtBottom(terminal.element));
    };

    terminal.element.addEventListener('scroll', handleTerminalScroll, {
      passive: true,
    });
    window.addEventListener('resize', handleViewportResize);
    window.visualViewport?.addEventListener('resize', handleViewportResize);
    scheduleTerminalSizeSync({ forceSend: true });
    handleTerminalScroll();

    return () => {
      resizeObserver.disconnect();
      terminal.element.removeEventListener('scroll', handleTerminalScroll);
      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleViewportResize);
      window.visualViewport?.removeEventListener(
        'resize',
        handleViewportResize
      );
    };
  }, [
    isTerminalReadyForSession,
    scheduleTerminalSizeSync,
    setTerminalFollowingOutput,
  ]);

    }

    if (!terminal || !isTerminalReadyForSession) {
      setConnectionStatus('connecting');
      return;
    }

    syncTerminalSize();
    const terminalSize = terminalSizeRef.current;
    let active = true;
    let reconnectTimer: number | undefined;
    let stableConnectionTimer: number | undefined;

    const clearReconnectTimer = () => {
      if (reconnectTimer === undefined) {
        return;
      }

      window.clearTimeout(reconnectTimer);
      reconnectTimer = undefined;
    };

    const clearStableConnectionTimer = () => {
      if (stableConnectionTimer === undefined) {
        return;
      }

      window.clearTimeout(stableConnectionTimer);
      stableConnectionTimer = undefined;
    };

    const markTerminalConnectionUseful = () => {
      clearStableConnectionTimer();
      reconnectAttemptRef.current = 0;
    };

    const markTerminalConnectionStableLater = () => {
      clearStableConnectionTimer();
      stableConnectionTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
        stableConnectionTimer = undefined;
        reconnectAttemptRef.current = 0;
      }, terminalStableConnectionMs);
    };

    const scheduleTerminalReconnect = () => {
      if (reconnectTimer !== undefined) {
        return;
      }

      const nextDelay = terminalReconnectDelaysMs[reconnectAttemptRef.current];
      if (nextDelay === undefined) {
        return;
      }

      reconnectAttemptRef.current += 1;
      reconnectTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
        reconnectTimer = undefined;
        clearBeforeNextMessageRef.current = true;
        setConnectionAttempt((currentAttempt) => currentAttempt + 1);
      }, nextDelay);
    };

    const socket = new WebSocket(
      createTerminalWebSocketURL(
        { id: sessionId, project: sessionProject },
        window.location,
        terminalSize
      )
    );
    socket.binaryType = 'arraybuffer';
    socketRef.current = socket;
    setConnectionStatus('connecting');

    socket.addEventListener('open', () => {
      if (!active) {
        return;
      }

      clearReconnectTimer();
      markTerminalConnectionStableLater();
      setConnectionStatus('connected');
      syncTerminalSize({ forceSend: true });
    });

    socket.addEventListener('message', (event) => {
      if (!active) {
        return;
      }

      markTerminalConnectionUseful();
      const currentTerminal = terminalRef.current;
      if (!currentTerminal) {
        return;
      }
      const shouldFollowOutput =
        isFollowingOutputRef.current &&
        isTerminalAtBottom(currentTerminal.element);

      if (clearBeforeNextMessageRef.current) {
        currentTerminal.write(terminalResetSequence);
        clearBeforeNextMessageRef.current = false;
      }
      writeTerminalMessage(currentTerminal, event.data);
      if (pendingTerminalFollowFrameRef.current !== undefined) {
        window.cancelAnimationFrame(pendingTerminalFollowFrameRef.current);
      }
      pendingTerminalFollowFrameRef.current = window.requestAnimationFrame(
        () => {
          pendingTerminalFollowFrameRef.current = undefined;
          const activeTerminal = terminalRef.current;
          if (
            !activeTerminal ||
            activeTerminal !== currentTerminal ||
            !active
          ) {
            return;
          }

          if (shouldFollowOutput) {
            scrollTerminalToBottom(activeTerminal.element);
            setTerminalFollowingOutput(true);
            return;
          }

          setTerminalFollowingOutput(
            isTerminalAtBottom(activeTerminal.element)
          );
        }
      );
    });

    socket.addEventListener('error', () => {
      if (active) {
        clearStableConnectionTimer();
        setConnectionStatus('failed');
        scheduleTerminalReconnect();
      }
    });

    socket.addEventListener('close', (event) => {
      if (!active) {
        return;
      }

      if (event.wasClean) {
        clearStableConnectionTimer();
        setConnectionStatus('disconnected');
        return;
      }

      clearStableConnectionTimer();
      setConnectionStatus('failed');
      scheduleTerminalReconnect();
    });

    return () => {
      active = false;
      if (socketRef.current === socket) {
        socketRef.current = null;
      }

      clearStableConnectionTimer();
      clearReconnectTimer();
      socket.close(1000, 'terminal session changed');
    };
  }, [
    connectionAttempt,
    isTerminalReadyForSession,
    sessionId,
    sessionProject,
    sessionTerminalSupported,
    setTerminalFollowingOutput,
    syncTerminalSize,
  ]);

  const handleTerminalReady = useCallback(
    (terminal: WTerm) => {
      terminalRef.current = terminal;
      setTerminalFollowingOutput(true);
      scrollTerminalToBottom(terminal.element);

      if (isTerminalReadyForSession) {
        scheduleTerminalSizeSync({ forceSend: true });
        return;
      }

      const measuredSize = measureTerminalSize(terminal.element);
      if (measuredSize) {
        terminalSizeRef.current = measuredSize;
        if (
          terminal.cols !== measuredSize.cols ||
          terminal.rows !== measuredSize.rows
        ) {
          terminal.resize(measuredSize.cols, measuredSize.rows);
        }
      }
      setReadyTerminalSessionKey(terminalSessionKey);
    },
    [
      isTerminalReadyForSession,
      scheduleTerminalSizeSync,
      setTerminalFollowingOutput,
      terminalSessionKey,
    ]
  );

  const handleTerminalData = useCallback((data: string) => {
    const socket = socketRef.current;
    if (!socket || socket.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) {
      return;
    }

    socket.send(textEncoder.encode(data));
  }, []);

  const handleTerminalResize = useCallback(
    (cols: number, rows: number) => {
      applyTerminalSize({ cols, rows });
    },
    [applyTerminalSize]
  );

  const handleTerminalError = useCallback((error: unknown) => {
    console.error('wterm failed to initialize', error);
    setConnectionStatus('failed');
  }, []);

  const handleTerminalRetry = useCallback(() => {
    clearBeforeNextMessageRef.current = true;
    reconnectAttemptRef.current = 0;
    setConnectionAttempt((currentAttempt) => currentAttempt + 1);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!sessionId || !sessionWorkerId) {
      lastConnectionToastRef.current = undefined;
      return;
    }

    const toastKey = `${sessionId}:${connectionAttempt}:${connectionStatus}`;
    if (lastConnectionToastRef.current === toastKey) {
      return;
    }

    switch (connectionStatus) {
      case 'unsupported':
        toast.warning('Terminal unavailable', {
          description: `${sessionWorkerId} does not expose an attachable runtime.`,
        });
        lastConnectionToastRef.current = toastKey;
        return;
      case 'disconnected':
        toast.warning('Terminal disconnected', {
          action: {
            label: 'Reconnect',
            onClick: handleTerminalRetry,
          },
          description: `${sessionWorkerId} stopped streaming terminal output.`,
        });
        lastConnectionToastRef.current = toastKey;
        return;
      case 'failed':
        toast.error('Terminal connection failed', {
          action: {
            label: 'Reconnect',
            onClick: handleTerminalRetry,
          },
          description: `${sessionWorkerId} could not attach to the worker runtime.`,
        });
        lastConnectionToastRef.current = toastKey;
        return;
      default:
        return;
    }
  }, [
    connectionAttempt,
    connectionStatus,
    handleTerminalRetry,
    sessionId,
    sessionWorkerId,
  ]);

  const handleTerminalFullscreenToggle = useCallback(() => {
    const terminalPanel = terminalPanelRef.current;

    if (!terminalPanel) {
      return;
    }

    if (document.fullscreenElement === terminalPanel) {
      void document.exitFullscreen();
      return;
    }

    void terminalPanel.requestFullscreen();
  }, []);

  const handleTerminalFontSizeDecrease = useCallback(() => {
    setTerminalFontSize((currentValue) =>
      normalizeTerminalFontSize(currentValue - 1)
    );
  }, []);

  const handleTerminalFontSizeIncrease = useCallback(() => {
    setTerminalFontSize((currentValue) =>
      normalizeTerminalFontSize(currentValue + 1)
    );
  }, []);

  const handleTerminalJumpToLatest = useCallback(() => {
    const terminal = terminalRef.current;
    if (!terminal) {
      return;
    }

    scrollTerminalToBottom(terminal.element);
    setTerminalFollowingOutput(true);
    terminal.focus();
  }, [setTerminalFollowingOutput]);

  const terminalLabel = session
    ? session.kind === 'orchestrator'
      ? 'Orchestrator terminal'
      : `${session.workerId} terminal`
    : 'Worker terminal';
  const fullscreenButtonLabel = isTerminalFullscreen
    ? 'Restore terminal'
    : 'Maximize terminal';
  const canRetry = isRetryableTerminalStatus(connectionStatus);
  const terminalStyle = {
    '--term-font-size': `${terminalFontSize}px`,
  } as CSSProperties;

  return (
    <section
      ref={terminalPanelRef}
      aria-label={`${terminalLabel} panel`}
      aria-hidden={props.hidden ? true : undefined}
      data-acceptance-anchor="terminal-panel"
      hidden={props.hidden}
      className={cn(
        'relative flex min-h-90 min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col border-b border-border bg-background md:min-h-0 md:border-b-0',
        isTerminalFullscreen && 'h-dvh w-dvw border-b-0',
        props.className

}

function getInitialTerminalFontSize() {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
    return terminalDefaultFontSize;
  }

  try {
    const savedValue = window.localStorage.getItem(terminalFontSizeStorageKey);
    if (savedValue === null) {
      return terminalDefaultFontSize;
    }

    return normalizeTerminalFontSize(Number(savedValue));
  } catch {
    return terminalDefaultFontSize;
  }
}

function normalizeTerminalFontSize(value: number) {
  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {
    return terminalDefaultFontSize;
  }

  return Math.min(
    terminalMaxFontSize,
    Math.max(terminalMinFontSize, Math.round(value))
  );
}

function isTerminalAtBottom(element: HTMLElement) {
  return (
    element.scrollHeight - element.clientHeight - element.scrollTop <=
    terminalFollowThresholdPx
  );
}

function scrollTerminalToBottom(element: HTMLElement) {
  element.scrollTop = element.scrollHeight;
}

function getTerminalStatusLabel(status: TerminalConnectionStatus) {
  switch (status) {
    case 'connected':
      return 'Connected';
    case 'connecting':
      return 'Connecting';
    case 'disconnected':
      return 'Disconnected';
    case 'failed':
      return 'Connection failed';
    case 'unsupported':
      return 'Terminal unavailable';
    case 'idle':
      return 'Idle';
  }
}

function getTerminalStatusClassName(status: TerminalConnectionStatus) {
  switch (status) {
    case 'connected':
      return 'bg-emerald-500';
    case 'connecting':
      return 'animate-pulse bg-amber-400';
    case 'disconnected':
    case 'failed':
      return 'bg-red-500';
    case 'unsupported':
      return 'bg-muted-foreground';
    case 'idle':
      return 'bg-muted-foreground/70';
  }
}

function measureTerminalSize(element: HTMLElement): TerminalSize | undefined {
  const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
  if (rect.width <= 0 || rect.height <= 0) {
    return undefined;
  }

  const row = document.createElement('div');
  row.className = 'term-row';
  row.style.position = 'absolute';
  row.style.visibility = 'hidden';
  row.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
  row.style.whiteSpace = 'pre';
  row.style.top = '0';
  row.style.left = '0';

  const probe = document.createElement('span');
  probe.textContent = 'W';
  row.appendChild(probe);
  element.appendChild(row);

  const charWidth = probe.getBoundingClientRect().width;
  const rowHeight = row.getBoundingClientRect().height;
  row.remove();

  if (charWidth <= 0 || rowHeight <= 0) {
    return undefined;
  }

  const style = getComputedStyle(element);
  const paddingX =
    toPixelNumber(style.paddingLeft) + toPixelNumber(style.paddingRight);
  const paddingY =
    toPixelNumber(style.paddingTop) + toPixelNumber(style.paddingBottom);
  const availableWidth = element.clientWidth - paddingX;
  const availableHeight = element.clientHeight - paddingY;

  if (availableWidth <= 0 || availableHeight <= 0) {
    return undefined;
  }

  return {
    cols: Math.max(1, Math.floor(availableWidth / charWidth)),
    rows: Math.max(1, Math.floor(availableHeight / rowHeight)),
  };
}

function toPixelNumber(value: string) {
  const parsed = Number.parseFloat(value);
  return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : 0;
}

function IconButton(props: {
  label: string;
  children: ReactNode;
  disabled?: boolean;
  onClick?: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <Tooltip>
      <TooltipTrigger
        render={
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="secondary"
            size="icon"
            className="size-9 rounded-none shadow-none"
            aria-label={props.label}
            disabled={props.disabled}
            onClick={props.onClick}
          />
        }
      >
        {props.children}
      </TooltipTrigger>
      <TooltipContent>
        <p>{props.label}</p>
      </TooltipContent>
    </Tooltip>
  );
}

function writeTerminalMessage(terminal: WTerm, data: unknown) {
  if (typeof data === 'string') {
    terminal.write(data);
    return;
  }

  if (data instanceof ArrayBuffer) {
    terminal.write(new Uint8Array(data));
    return;
  }

  if (data instanceof Blob) {
    void data.arrayBuffer().then((buffer) => {
      terminal.write(new Uint8Array(buffer));
    });
  }
}

   229	- `ao events list --source ui --since 12h --limit 300 --json` already contained `ui.terminal_pty_lost` with `exitCode: 1`, `reattachSkipped: true`, and `tmuxSessionPresent: false`, which is strong evidence for the server’s “session gone, do not re-attach” path [Task 1]

  5877	- `MuxProvider.tsx` has its own terminal lifecycle (`opened`, `exited`, `error`) that is separate from `session.status`; terminal attachability should not be derived from `TERMINAL_STATUSES.has(session.status)` alone [Task 1][Task 2]

  5880	- The validated UI behavior is: terminal `error` events keep the terminal mounted, while only `exited` events switch to the generic ended placeholder [Task 2]
  5881	- `DirectTerminal.tsx` already had the xterm stylesheet import restored in the live tree during the review-fix run, so later regressions there should not start by re-fixing that import blindly [Task 2]

Fix: trace `SessionDetail`, `MuxProvider`, and `mux-websocket.ts` together, and gate the placeholder on terminal lifecycle state rather than session status alone [Task 1][Task 2]

   720	function getInitialTerminalFontSize() {
   721	  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
   722	    return terminalDefaultFontSize;
   723	  }
   724	
   725	  try {
   726	    const savedValue = window.localStorage.getItem(terminalFontSizeStorageKey);
   727	    if (savedValue === null) {
   728	      return terminalDefaultFontSize;
   729	    }
   730	
   731	    return normalizeTerminalFontSize(Number(savedValue));
   732	  } catch {
   733	    return terminalDefaultFontSize;
   734	  }
   735	}

> AGENT

Implemented the first terminal viewer parity pass.

Changed [terminal-panel.tsx](/Users/tanishqpalandurkar/Projects/better-ao/web/src/features/home/components/organisms/terminal-panel.tsx:365) to add AO-like viewer basics: visible connection status, persisted font-size controls, follow-output tracking, and a “Jump to latest” control when the user scrolls away from live output. The terminal now preserves manual scroll position until explicitly re-anchored.

Added coverage in [root.spec.ts](/Users/tanishqpalandurkar/Projects/better-ao/web/e2e/root.spec.ts:1331) for jump-to-latest and [root.spec.ts](/Users/tanishqpalandurkar/Projects/better-ao/web/e2e/root.spec.ts:1428) for font-size persistence. I also throttled the terminal test output helper so big fake backend writes do not flood wterm under parallel E2E load.

Validation passed:
- `pnpm --dir web lint:ts`
- `pnpm --dir web exec playwright test root.spec.ts`
- `pnpm --dir web exec playwright test root.spec.ts -g "terminal can jump|terminal font size"`

Remaining parity work is the deeper AO side: durable terminal lifecycle/mux behavior and session attach semantics. This pass stays on the viewer surface only.

import { Terminal, type WTerm } from '@wterm/react';
import {
  ArrowDownIcon,
  Maximize2Icon,
  Minimize2Icon,
  MinusIcon,
  PlusIcon,
  RotateCcwIcon,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  type CSSProperties,
  type ReactNode,
  useCallback,
  useEffect,
  useLayoutEffect,
  useRef,
  useState,
} from 'react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

import { cn } from '@/lib/tailwind/utils';

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import {
  Tooltip,
  TooltipContent,
  TooltipTrigger,
} from '@/components/ui/tooltip';

import { OpenIdeButton } from '@/features/home/components/molecules/open-ide-button';
import {
  createTerminalWebSocketURL,
  initialTerminalSize,
  isRetryableTerminalStatus,
  type TerminalConnectionStatus,
} from '@/features/home/components/organisms/terminal-connection';
import type { WorkerSession } from '@/features/home/domain/session-workspace';

const textEncoder = new TextEncoder();
const terminalReconnectDelaysMs = [500, 1_000, 2_000] as const;
const terminalResetSequence = '\x1b[3J\x1b[2J\x1b[H';
const terminalStableConnectionMs = 5_000;
const terminalFontSizeStorageKey = 'better-ao.terminal.fontSize';
const terminalDefaultFontSize = 12;
const terminalMinFontSize = 10;
const terminalMaxFontSize = 18;
const terminalFollowThresholdPx = 8;

type TerminalSize = typeof initialTerminalSize;

export function TerminalPanel(props: {
  className?: string;
  hidden?: boolean;
  session?: WorkerSession;
}) {
  const { session } = props;
  const socketRef = useRef<WebSocket | null>(null);
  const terminalRef = useRef<WTerm | null>(null);
  const terminalPanelRef = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null);
  const terminalViewportRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const clearBeforeNextMessageRef = useRef(false);
  const reconnectAttemptRef = useRef(0);
  const pendingTerminalResizeFrameRef = useRef<number | undefined>(undefined);
  const pendingTerminalResizeForceRef = useRef(false);
  const pendingTerminalFollowFrameRef = useRef<number | undefined>(undefined);
  const terminalSizeRef = useRef(initialTerminalSize);
  const lastConnectionToastRef = useRef<string | undefined>(undefined);
  const isFollowingOutputRef = useRef(true);
  const [readyTerminalSessionKey, setReadyTerminalSessionKey] = useState<
    string | undefined
  >(undefined);
  const [connectionStatus, setConnectionStatus] =
    useState<TerminalConnectionStatus>('idle');
  const [connectionAttempt, setConnectionAttempt] = useState(0);
  const [isTerminalFullscreen, setIsTerminalFullscreen] = useState(false);
  const [terminalFontSize, setTerminalFontSize] = useState(
    getInitialTerminalFontSize
  );
  const [isFollowingOutput, setIsFollowingOutput] = useState(true);

  const sendResize = useCallback((cols: number, rows: number) => {
    const socket = socketRef.current;
    if (!socket || socket.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) {
      return;
    }

    socket.send(
      JSON.stringify({
        cols,
        rows,
        type: 'resize',
      })
    );
  }, []);

  const terminalSessionKey = session
    ? `${session.project}/${session.id}`
    : 'idle';
  const sessionId = session?.id;
  const sessionProject = session?.project;
  const sessionTerminalSupported = session?.terminalSupported;
  const sessionWorkerId = session?.workerId;
  const isTerminalReadyForSession =
    readyTerminalSessionKey === terminalSessionKey;

  const applyTerminalSize = useCallback(
    (size: TerminalSize, options?: { forceSend?: boolean }) => {
      const previousSize = terminalSizeRef.current;
      terminalSizeRef.current = size;

      const terminal = terminalRef.current;
      if (
        terminal &&
        isTerminalReadyForSession &&
        (terminal.cols !== size.cols || terminal.rows !== size.rows)
      ) {
        terminal.resize(size.cols, size.rows);
      }

      if (
        options?.forceSend ||
        previousSize.cols !== size.cols ||
        previousSize.rows !== size.rows
      ) {
        sendResize(size.cols, size.rows);
      }
    },
    [isTerminalReadyForSession, sendResize]
  );

  const syncTerminalSize = useCallback(
    (options?: { forceSend?: boolean }) => {
      const terminal = terminalRef.current;
      if (!terminal || !isTerminalReadyForSession) {
        return false;
      }

      const measuredSize = measureTerminalSize(terminal.element);
      if (!measuredSize) {
        return false;
      }

      applyTerminalSize(measuredSize, options);
      return true;
    },
    [applyTerminalSize, isTerminalReadyForSession]
  );

  const scheduleTerminalSizeSync = useCallback(
    (options?: { forceSend?: boolean }) => {
      pendingTerminalResizeForceRef.current =
        pendingTerminalResizeForceRef.current || Boolean(options?.forceSend);

      if (pendingTerminalResizeFrameRef.current !== undefined) {
        return;
      }

      pendingTerminalResizeFrameRef.current = window.requestAnimationFrame(
        () => {
          pendingTerminalResizeFrameRef.current = undefined;
          const forceSend = pendingTerminalResizeForceRef.current;
          pendingTerminalResizeForceRef.current = false;
          syncTerminalSize({ forceSend });
        }
      );
    },
    [syncTerminalSize]
  );

  const setTerminalFollowingOutput = useCallback((nextValue: boolean) => {
    isFollowingOutputRef.current = nextValue;
    setIsFollowingOutput((currentValue) =>
      currentValue === nextValue ? currentValue : nextValue
    );
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleFullscreenChange = () => {
      setIsTerminalFullscreen(
        document.fullscreenElement === terminalPanelRef.current
      );

      scheduleTerminalSizeSync({ forceSend: true });
    };

    document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', handleFullscreenChange);

    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('fullscreenchange', handleFullscreenChange);
    };
  }, [scheduleTerminalSizeSync]);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const terminal = terminalRef.current;
    if (!terminal || !isTerminalReadyForSession) {
      return;
    }

    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => {
      scheduleTerminalSizeSync();
    });
    resizeObserver.observe(terminal.element);

    const terminalViewport = terminalViewportRef.current;
    if (terminalViewport) {
      resizeObserver.observe(terminalViewport);
    }

    const handleViewportResize = () => {
      scheduleTerminalSizeSync();
    };

    const handleTerminalScroll = () => {
      setTerminalFollowingOutput(isTerminalAtBottom(terminal.element));
    };

    terminal.element.addEventListener('scroll', handleTerminalScroll, {
      passive: true,
    });
    window.addEventListener('resize', handleViewportResize);
    window.visualViewport?.addEventListener('resize', handleViewportResize);
    scheduleTerminalSizeSync({ forceSend: true });
    handleTerminalScroll();

    return () => {
      resizeObserver.disconnect();
      terminal.element.removeEventListener('scroll', handleTerminalScroll);
      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleViewportResize);
      window.visualViewport?.removeEventListener(
        'resize',
        handleViewportResize
      );
    };
  }, [
    isTerminalReadyForSession,
    scheduleTerminalSizeSync,
    setTerminalFollowingOutput,
  ]);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (pendingTerminalResizeFrameRef.current !== undefined) {
        window.cancelAnimationFrame(pendingTerminalResizeFrameRef.current);
        pendingTerminalResizeFrameRef.current = undefined;
      }
      if (pendingTerminalFollowFrameRef.current !== undefined) {
        window.cancelAnimationFrame(pendingTerminalFollowFrameRef.current);
        pendingTerminalFollowFrameRef.current = undefined;
      }
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      window.localStorage.setItem(
        terminalFontSizeStorageKey,
        String(terminalFontSize)
      );
    } catch {
      // Terminal sizing still works without persisted browser storage.
    }
    scheduleTerminalSizeSync({ forceSend: true });
  }, [scheduleTerminalSizeSync, terminalFontSize]);

  useEffect(() => {
    clearBeforeNextMessageRef.current = false;
    reconnectAttemptRef.current = 0;
    setTerminalFollowingOutput(true);
  }, [setTerminalFollowingOutput, terminalSessionKey]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const terminal = terminalRef.current;
    if (!sessionId || !sessionProject) {
      setConnectionStatus('idle');
      return;
    }

    if (!sessionTerminalSupported) {
      setConnectionStatus('unsupported');
      return;
    }

    if (!terminal || !isTerminalReadyForSession) {
      setConnectionStatus('connecting');
      return;
    }

    syncTerminalSize();
    const terminalSize = terminalSizeRef.current;
    let active = true;
    let reconnectTimer: number | undefined;
    let stableConnectionTimer: number | undefined;

    const clearReconnectTimer = () => {
      if (reconnectTimer === undefined) {
        return;
      }

      window.clearTimeout(reconnectTimer);
      reconnectTimer = undefined;
    };

    const clearStableConnectionTimer = () => {
      if (stableConnectionTimer === undefined) {
        return;
      }

      window.clearTimeout(stableConnectionTimer);
      stableConnectionTimer = undefined;
    };

    const markTerminalConnectionUseful = () => {
      clearStableConnectionTimer();
      reconnectAttemptRef.current = 0;
    };

    const markTerminalConnectionStableLater = () => {
      clearStableConnectionTimer();
      stableConnectionTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
        stableConnectionTimer = undefined;
        reconnectAttemptRef.current = 0;
      }, terminalStableConnectionMs);
    };

    const scheduleTerminalReconnect = () => {
      if (reconnectTimer !== undefined) {
        return;
      }

      const nextDelay = terminalReconnectDelaysMs[reconnectAttemptRef.current];
      if (nextDelay === undefined) {
        return;
      }

      reconnectAttemptRef.current += 1;
      reconnectTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
        reconnectTimer = undefined;
        clearBeforeNextMessageRef.current = true;
        setConnectionAttempt((currentAttempt) => currentAttempt + 1);
      }, nextDelay);
    };

    const socket = new WebSocket(
      createTerminalWebSocketURL(
        { id: sessionId, project: sessionProject },
        window.location,
        terminalSize
      )
    );
    socket.binaryType = 'arraybuffer';
    socketRef.current = socket;
    setConnectionStatus('connecting');

    socket.addEventListener('open', () => {
      if (!active) {
        return;
      }

      clearReconnectTimer();
      markTerminalConnectionStableLater();
      setConnectionStatus('connected');
      syncTerminalSize({ forceSend: true });
    });

    socket.addEventListener('message', (event) => {
      if (!active) {
        return;
      }

      markTerminalConnectionUseful();
      const currentTerminal = terminalRef.current;
      if (!currentTerminal) {
        return;
      }
      const shouldFollowOutput =
        isFollowingOutputRef.current &&
        isTerminalAtBottom(currentTerminal.element);

      if (clearBeforeNextMessageRef.current) {
        currentTerminal.write(terminalResetSequence);
        clearBeforeNextMessageRef.current = false;
      }
      writeTerminalMessage(currentTerminal, event.data);
      if (pendingTerminalFollowFrameRef.current !== undefined) {
        window.cancelAnimationFrame(pendingTerminalFollowFrameRef.current);
      }
      pendingTerminalFollowFrameRef.current = window.requestAnimationFrame(
        () => {
          pendingTerminalFollowFrameRef.current = undefined;
          const activeTerminal = terminalRef.current;
          if (
            !activeTerminal ||
            activeTerminal !== currentTerminal ||
            !active
          ) {
            return;
          }

          if (shouldFollowOutput) {
            scrollTerminalToBottom(activeTerminal.element);
            setTerminalFollowingOutput(true);
            return;
          }

          setTerminalFollowingOutput(
            isTerminalAtBottom(activeTerminal.element)
          );
        }
      );
    });

    socket.addEventListener('error', () => {
      if (active) {
        clearStableConnectionTimer();
        setConnectionStatus('failed');
        scheduleTerminalReconnect();
      }
    });

    socket.addEventListener('close', (event) => {
      if (!active) {
        return;
      }

      if (event.wasClean) {
        clearStableConnectionTimer();
        setConnectionStatus('disconnected');
        return;
      }

      clearStableConnectionTimer();
      setConnectionStatus('failed');
      scheduleTerminalReconnect();
    });

    return () => {
      active = false;
      if (socketRef.current === socket) {
        socketRef.current = null;
      }

      clearStableConnectionTimer();
      clearReconnectTimer();
      socket.close(1000, 'terminal session changed');
    };
  }, [
    connectionAttempt,
    isTerminalReadyForSession,
    sessionId,
    sessionProject,
    sessionTerminalSupported,
    setTerminalFollowingOutput,
    syncTerminalSize,
  ]);

  const handleTerminalReady = useCallback(
    (terminal: WTerm) => {
      terminalRef.current = terminal;
      setTerminalFollowingOutput(true);
      scrollTerminalToBottom(terminal.element);

      if (isTerminalReadyForSession) {
        scheduleTerminalSizeSync({ forceSend: true });
        return;
      }

      const measuredSize = measureTerminalSize(terminal.element);
      if (measuredSize) {
        terminalSizeRef.current = measuredSize;
        if (
          terminal.cols !== measuredSize.cols ||
          terminal.rows !== measuredSize.rows
        ) {
          terminal.resize(measuredSize.cols, measuredSize.rows);
        }
      }
      setReadyTerminalSessionKey(terminalSessionKey);
    },
    [
      isTerminalReadyForSession,
      scheduleTerminalSizeSync,
      setTerminalFollowingOutput,
      terminalSessionKey,
    ]
  );

  const handleTerminalData = useCallback((data: string) => {
    const socket = socketRef.current;
    if (!socket || socket.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) {
      return;
    }

    socket.send(textEncoder.encode(data));
  }, []);

  const handleTerminalResize = useCallback(
    (cols: number, rows: number) => {
      applyTerminalSize({ cols, rows });
    },
    [applyTerminalSize]
  );

  const handleTerminalError = useCallback((error: unknown) => {
    console.error('wterm failed to initialize', error);
    setConnectionStatus('failed');
  }, []);

  const handleTerminalRetry = useCallback(() => {
    clearBeforeNextMessageRef.current = true;
    reconnectAttemptRef.current = 0;
    setConnectionAttempt((currentAttempt) => currentAttempt + 1);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!sessionId || !sessionWorkerId) {
      lastConnectionToastRef.current = undefined;
      return;
    }

    const toastKey = `${sessionId}:${connectionAttempt}:${connectionStatus}`;
    if (lastConnectionToastRef.current === toastKey) {
      return;
    }

    switch (connectionStatus) {
      case 'unsupported':
        toast.warning('Terminal unavailable', {
          description: `${sessionWorkerId} does not expose an attachable runtime.`,
        });
        lastConnectionToastRef.current = toastKey;
        return;
      case 'disconnected':
        toast.warning('Terminal disconnected', {
          action: {
            label: 'Reconnect',
            onClick: handleTerminalRetry,
          },
          description: `${sessionWorkerId} stopped streaming terminal output.`,
        });
        lastConnectionToastRef.current = toastKey;
        return;
      case 'failed':
        toast.error('Terminal connection failed', {
          action: {
            label: 'Reconnect',
            onClick: handleTerminalRetry,
          },
          description: `${sessionWorkerId} could not attach to the worker runtime.`,
        });
        lastConnectionToastRef.current = toastKey;
        return;
      default:
        return;
    }
  }, [
    connectionAttempt,
    connectionStatus,
    handleTerminalRetry,
    sessionId,
    sessionWorkerId,
  ]);

  const handleTerminalFullscreenToggle = useCallback(() => {
    const terminalPanel = terminalPanelRef.current;

    if (!terminalPanel) {
      return;
    }

    if (document.fullscreenElement === terminalPanel) {
      void document.exitFullscreen();
      return;
    }

    void terminalPanel.requestFullscreen();
  }, []);

  const handleTerminalFontSizeDecrease = useCallback(() => {
    setTerminalFontSize((currentValue) =>
      normalizeTerminalFontSize(currentValue - 1)
    );
  }, []);

  const handleTerminalFontSizeIncrease = useCallback(() => {
    setTerminalFontSize((currentValue) =>
      normalizeTerminalFontSize(currentValue + 1)
    );
  }, []);

  const handleTerminalJumpToLatest = useCallback(() => {
    const terminal = terminalRef.current;
    if (!terminal) {
      return;
    }

    scrollTerminalToBottom(terminal.element);
    setTerminalFollowingOutput(true);
    terminal.focus();
  }, [setTerminalFollowingOutput]);

  const terminalLabel = session
    ? session.kind === 'orchestrator'
      ? 'Orchestrator terminal'
      : `${session.workerId} terminal`
    : 'Worker terminal';
  const fullscreenButtonLabel = isTerminalFullscreen
    ? 'Restore terminal'
    : 'Maximize terminal';
  const canRetry = isRetryableTerminalStatus(connectionStatus);
  const terminalStyle = {
    '--term-font-size': `${terminalFontSize}px`,
  } as CSSProperties;

  return (
    <section
      ref={terminalPanelRef}
      aria-label={`${terminalLabel} panel`}
      aria-hidden={props.hidden ? true : undefined}
      data-acceptance-anchor="terminal-panel"
      hidden={props.hidden}
      className={cn(
        'relative flex min-h-90 min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col border-b border-border bg-background md:min-h-0 md:border-b-0',
        isTerminalFullscreen && 'h-dvh w-dvw border-b-0',
        props.className
      )}
    >
      <div className="flex h-10 shrink-0 items-center gap-2 border-b border-border bg-background px-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2">
          <span
            aria-hidden="true"
            className={cn(
              'size-2 shrink-0 rounded-full',
              getTerminalStatusClassName(connectionStatus)
            )}
          />
          <span className="truncate font-medium text-foreground">
            {sessionId ?? 'Terminal'}
          </span>
          <span className="hidden shrink-0 sm:inline">
            {getTerminalStatusLabel(connectionStatus)}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="ml-auto flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1">
          <IconButton
            disabled={terminalFontSize <= terminalMinFontSize}
            label="Decrease terminal font size"
            onClick={handleTerminalFontSizeDecrease}
          >
            <MinusIcon />
          </IconButton>
          <span
            aria-label="Terminal font size"
            className="w-10 text-center text-xs tabular-nums"
          >
            {terminalFontSize}px
          </span>
          <IconButton
            disabled={terminalFontSize >= terminalMaxFontSize}
            label="Increase terminal font size"
            onClick={handleTerminalFontSizeIncrease}
          >
            <PlusIcon />
          </IconButton>
          {canRetry ? (
            <IconButton
              label="Reconnect terminal"
              onClick={handleTerminalRetry}
            >
              <RotateCcwIcon />
            </IconButton>
          ) : null}
          <OpenIdeButton session={session} />
          <IconButton
            label={fullscreenButtonLabel}
            onClick={handleTerminalFullscreenToggle}
          >
            {isTerminalFullscreen ? <Minimize2Icon /> : <Maximize2Icon />}
          </IconButton>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div
        ref={terminalViewportRef}
        className={cn(
          'relative min-h-0 flex-1 bg-background p-3',
          isTerminalFullscreen && 'p-0'
        )}
      >
        <Terminal
          key={terminalSessionKey}
          aria-label={terminalLabel}
          autoResize
          className="ao-terminal"
          cols={initialTerminalSize.cols}
          cursorBlink
          onData={handleTerminalData}
          onError={handleTerminalError}
          onReady={handleTerminalReady}
          onResize={handleTerminalResize}
          rows={initialTerminalSize.rows}
          style={terminalStyle}
          wasmUrl="/wterm.wasm"
        />
        {!isFollowingOutput ? (
          <div className="absolute right-6 bottom-6 z-10">
            <IconButton
              label="Jump to latest"
              onClick={handleTerminalJumpToLatest}
            >
              <ArrowDownIcon />
            </IconButton>
          </div>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function getInitialTerminalFontSize() {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
    return terminalDefaultFontSize;
  }

  try {
    const savedValue = window.localStorage.getItem(terminalFontSizeStorageKey);
    if (savedValue === null) {
      return terminalDefaultFontSize;
    }

    return normalizeTerminalFontSize(Number(savedValue));
  } catch {
    return terminalDefaultFontSize;
  }
}

function normalizeTerminalFontSize(value: number) {
  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {
    return terminalDefaultFontSize;
  }

  return Math.min(
    terminalMaxFontSize,
    Math.max(terminalMinFontSize, Math.round(value))
  );
}

function isTerminalAtBottom(element: HTMLElement) {
  return (
    element.scrollHeight - element.clientHeight - element.scrollTop <=
    terminalFollowThresholdPx
  );
}

function scrollTerminalToBottom(element: HTMLElement) {
  element.scrollTop = element.scrollHeight;
}

function getTerminalStatusLabel(status: TerminalConnectionStatus) {
  switch (status) {
    case 'connected':
      return 'Connected';
    case 'connecting':
      return 'Connecting';
    case 'disconnected':
      return 'Disconnected';
    case 'failed':
      return 'Connection failed';
    case 'unsupported':
      return 'Terminal unavailable';
    case 'idle':
      return 'Idle';
  }
}

function getTerminalStatusClassName(status: TerminalConnectionStatus) {
  switch (status) {
    case 'connected':
      return 'bg-emerald-500';
    case 'connecting':
      return 'animate-pulse bg-amber-400';
    case 'disconnected':
    case 'failed':
      return 'bg-red-500';
    case 'unsupported':
      return 'bg-muted-foreground';
    case 'idle':
      return 'bg-muted-foreground/70';
  }
}

function measureTerminalSize(element: HTMLElement): TerminalSize | undefined {
  const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
  if (rect.width <= 0 || rect.height <= 0) {
    return undefined;
  }

  const row = document.createElement('div');
  row.className = 'term-row';
  row.style.position = 'absolute';
  row.style.visibility = 'hidden';
  row.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
  row.style.whiteSpace = 'pre';
  row.style.top = '0';
  row.style.left = '0';

  const probe = document.createElement('span');
  probe.textContent = 'W';
  row.appendChild(probe);
  element.appendChild(row);

  const charWidth = probe.getBoundingClientRect().width;
  const rowHeight = row.getBoundingClientRect().height;
  row.remove();

  if (charWidth <= 0 || rowHeight <= 0) {
    return undefined;
  }

  const style = getComputedStyle(element);
  const paddingX =
    toPixelNumber(style.paddingLeft) + toPixelNumber(style.paddingRight);
  const paddingY =
    toPixelNumber(style.paddingTop) + toPixelNumber(style.paddingBottom);
  const availableWidth = element.clientWidth - paddingX;
  const availableHeight = element.clientHeight - paddingY;

  if (availableWidth <= 0 || availableHeight <= 0) {
    return undefined;
  }

  return {
    cols: Math.max(1, Math.floor(availableWidth / charWidth)),
    rows: Math.max(1, Math.floor(availableHeight / rowHeight)),
  };
}

function toPixelNumber(value: string) {
  const parsed = Number.parseFloat(value);
  return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : 0;
}

function IconButton(props: {
  label: string;
  children: ReactNode;
  disabled?: boolean;
  onClick?: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <Tooltip>
      <TooltipTrigger
        render={
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="secondary"
            size="icon"
            className="size-9 rounded-none shadow-none"
            aria-label={props.label}
            disabled={props.disabled}
            onClick={props.onClick}
          />
        }
      >
        {props.children}
      </TooltipTrigger>
      <TooltipContent>
        <p>{props.label}</p>
      </TooltipContent>
    </Tooltip>
  );
}

function writeTerminalMessage(terminal: WTerm, data: unknown) {
  if (typeof data === 'string') {
    terminal.write(data);
    return;
  }

  if (data instanceof ArrayBuffer) {
    terminal.write(new Uint8Array(data));
    return;
  }

  if (data instanceof Blob) {
    void data.arrayBuffer().then((buffer) => {
      terminal.write(new Uint8Array(buffer));
    });
  }
}

async function sendTerminalBackendTextAndWait(
  page: Page,
  terminalPanel: Locator,
  text: string,
  expectedText: string
) {
  const terminal = terminalPanel.locator('.ao-terminal');
  let lastSendTime = 0;

  await expect
    .poll(
      async () => {
        const currentTime = Date.now();
        if (currentTime - lastSendTime > 500) {
          await sendTerminalBackendText(page, text);
          lastSendTime = currentTime;
        }
        await page.waitForTimeout(50);
        return (await terminal.textContent()) ?? '';
      },
      {
        intervals: [100, 250, 500],
        timeout: 15_000,
      }
    )
    .toContain(expectedText);
}

          url?: string;
        }
      >;
    };
    const socket = terminalWindow.__terminalWebSockets
      ?.filter(
    .toBe(true);

    element.scrollTop = 0;
  });
  await terminal.hover();
  await page.mouse.wheel(0, 400);
  await expect
    .poll(() => terminal.evaluate((element) => element.scrollTop))
    .toBeGreaterThan(0);
});

test('terminal can jump back to latest output after scrolling away', async ({
  page,
}) => {
  await installFakeTerminalWebSocket(page);
  await page.route('**/api/workspace', async (route) => {
    await route.fulfill({
      body: JSON.stringify({
        activeProjectId: 'agent-orchestrator_live',
        projects: [
          { id: 'agent-orchestrator_live', name: 'Agent Orchestrator' },
        ],
        sessions: [
          {
            agent: 'codex',
            cwd: '/tmp/ao-live',
            description: 'A selected live worker.',
            id: 'ao-live',
            issue: '[PR #1]',
            metadata: '[codex/working]',
            project: 'agent-orchestrator_live',
            selected: true,
            state: 'working',
            terminalSupported: true,
            title: 'Live worker',
            workerId: '[AO-LIVE]',
          },
        ],
      }),
      contentType: 'application/json',
      status: 200,
    });
  });

  await page.goto('/');
  await expect(
    page.getByRole('button', { name: '[AO-LIVE] Live worker' })
  ).toBeVisible({
    timeout: 15_000,
  });

  const scrollbackText = Array.from(
    { length: 120 },
    (_, index) => `LATEST_${index.toString().padStart(3, '0')}\r\n`
  ).join('');
  await page.evaluate((text) => {
    (
      window as Window & {
        __terminalInitialBackendText?: string;
      }
    ).__terminalInitialBackendText = text;
  }, scrollbackText);

  await page.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Terminal' }).click();
  const terminalPanel = page.locator(
    '[data-acceptance-anchor="terminal-panel"]'
  );
  const terminal = terminalPanel.getByRole('textbox');
  await expect(terminal).toBeVisible();
  await expect.poll(() => getTerminalOpenWebSocketCount(page)).not.toBe(0);
  await expect.poll(() => hasTerminalResizeControlMessage(page)).toBe(true);
  await expect(terminalPanel.locator('.ao-terminal')).toContainText(
    'LATEST_119',
    {
      timeout: 15_000,
    }
  );

  await expect
    .poll(() =>
      terminal.evaluate(
        (element) => element.scrollHeight > element.clientHeight
      )
    )
    .toBe(true);
  await terminal.evaluate((element) => {
    element.scrollTop = 0;
    element.dispatchEvent(new Event('scroll'));
  });

  await expect(
    terminalPanel.getByRole('button', { name: 'Jump to latest' })
  ).toBeVisible();
  await terminalPanel.getByRole('button', { name: 'Jump to latest' }).click();

  await expect
    .poll(() =>
      terminal.evaluate(
        (element) =>
          element.scrollHeight - element.clientHeight - element.scrollTop
      )
    )
    .toBeLessThanOrEqual(8);
  await expect(
    terminalPanel.getByRole('button', { name: 'Jump to latest' })
  ).toBeHidden();
});

      body: JSON.stringify({

        projects: [

        ],
        sessions: [
          {

            description: 'A selected live worker.',

            metadata: '[codex/working]',

            state: 'working',

            title: 'Live worker',
            workerId: '[AO-LIVE]',

        ],
      }),

      status: 200,
    });

  await page.goto('/');
  await expect(
    page.getByRole('button', { name: '[AO-LIVE] Live worker' })
  ).toBeVisible({
    timeout: 15_000,
  });

  const terminalPanel = page.locator(

  );

    .getByRole('button', { name: 'Increase terminal font size' })
    .click();
  await expect(terminalFontSize).toHaveText('13px');
  await expect
    .poll(() =>
      terminalPanel
        .locator('.ao-terminal')
        .evaluate((element) =>
          getComputedStyle(element).getPropertyValue('--term-font-size').trim()
        )
    )
    .toBe('13px');

  await page.reload();
  await expect(
    page.locator('[data-acceptance-anchor="terminal-panel"]')
  ).toBeVisible({

  });
  await page.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Terminal' }).click();
  await expect(
    page
      .locator('[data-acceptance-anchor="terminal-panel"]')
      .getByLabel('Terminal font size', { exact: true })
  ).toHaveText('13px');
});

test('terminal automatically reconnects after a transient websocket drop', async ({
  page,
}) => {
  await installFakeTerminalWebSocket(page);
  await page.route('**/api/workspace', async (route) => {
    await route.fulfill({
      body: JSON.stringify({
        activeProjectId: 'agent-orchestrator_live',
        projects: [
          { id: 'agent-orchestrator_live', name: 'Agent Orchestrator' },
        ],
        sessions: [
          {
            agent: 'codex',
            cwd: '/tmp/ao-live',
            description: 'A selected live worker.',
            id: 'ao-live',
            issue: '[PR #1]',
            metadata: '[codex/working]',
            project: 'agent-orchestrator_live',
            selected: true,
            state: 'working',
            terminalSupported: true,
            title: 'Live worker',
            workerId: '[AO-LIVE]',
          },
        ],
      }),
      contentType: 'application/json',
      status: 200,
    });
  });

  await page.goto('/');
  await expect(
    page.getByRole('button', { name: '[AO-LIVE] Live worker' })
  ).toBeVisible({
    timeout: 15_000,
  });

  await page.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Terminal' }).click();
  const terminalPanel = page.locator(
    '[data-acceptance-anchor="terminal-panel"]'
  );
  const terminal = terminalPanel.getByRole('textbox');
  await expect(terminal).toBeVisible();
  await expect.poll(() => getTerminalOpenWebSocketCount(page)).not.toBe(0);
  await expect.poll(() => hasTerminalResizeControlMessage(page)).toBe(true);
  const initialWebSocketCount = await getTerminalWebSocketCount(page);
  await sendTerminalBackendTextAndWait(
    page,
    terminalPanel,
    'BEFORE_DROP\r\n',
    'BEFORE_DROP'
  );

  await dropTerminalWebSocket(page);
  await expect
    .poll(() => getTerminalWebSocketCount(page))
    .toBeGreaterThan(initialWebSocketCount);
  await expect.poll(() => getTerminalOpenWebSocketCount(page)).not.toBe(0);
  await sendTerminalBackendTextAndWait(
    page,
    terminalPanel,
    'BEFORE_DROP\r\nAFTER_RECONNECT\r\n',
    'AFTER_RECONNECT'
  );
  await expect
    .poll(async () => {
      const text = (await terminalPanel.textContent()) ?? '';
      return text.match(/BEFORE_DROP/g)?.length ?? 0;
    })
    .toBe(1);
});
